## 8. Non-goals and limits

- No evolving against an archive, no recent-only/fixed-panel/mixture comparison, no withheld-opponent generality, no whole-patch or ecological-context transfer, no predation or reproduction claim, no open-endedness claim.
- Archive time is search evaluation index; archives are surviving elites, not a full history; sources are three search runs, so intervals are wide and "nothing" means none detected at this resolution.
- Biomass share at step 300 in a small CPU arena with a single chemistry; balanced arm only; no GPU or physical-hardware evidence.
- No physics, schema, preset, `RULE_VERSION` or `defaultConfig()` change; no new `WorldConfig` keys; no paid calls, AWS, deploy; the plan stage commits nothing.
- If scope must shrink, cut in this order: third source, `strength-not-time` in-world version (keep the pure matrix), the Playwright spec (keep render tests), residual-based statistic.

## 9. Order of work

1. Library with pure functions and tests (selection, permutation null, verdict, fixtures). 2. Snapshot selection and viability screen from `RUNS` into `archive.json` and `archive-config.json`. 3. CLI (`--freeze-check`, mini config). 4. Codex review of freeze files, fix, commit only freeze files, `git push -u origin claude/i03-archive-tournament`. 5. `--write` the evidence, `--replay-check`. 6. Page, packaging edits, render and e2e tests, screenshots. 7. `EVIDENCE.md` (result, deviations, cost), full gates, Codex source review.

## v3 pre-freeze amendment (2026-11-02, IMPLEMENT stage, before any scoring seed was run)

Made after chain calibration on screen seeds 901..903 only (`chain-calibration.json`, 7 trials) and before the freeze commit. No scoring seed (1001..1010) had been run.

1. **Planted-chain gate (section 2.2 and verdict row 3).** v2 required the planted chain to be `directional-rank`. Six of seven calibration trials reached `r_dir` at the K=4 floor (1/24; the seventh, `[70,45,20,0]`, was 2/24 and `nothing-beyond-null`) and none had an aligned vigour residual (residual ranks 0.29 to 0.67), because the planted manipulation (a weakened photosynthesis weight) is a vigour manipulation by construction. The gate is now: planted chain is `directional-rank` or `directional-vigour-only` (it must be directional; the vigour split is gated by the `vigour-only` fixture). Selected chain: weakening percents `[80,60,40,0]`, time in ascending strength.
2. **Stated consequence.** No fixture or control reaches `directional-rank` (strength-aligned on the first rung's panel is also `directional-vigour-only`, residual rank 0.092). The top label has no positive control; a real `directional-rank` verdict would be unvalidated and is recorded as a limit in the evidence.
3. **strength-not-time in-world rerun is cut** (allowed by section 8's cut order); the pure-matrix fixture on the first rung's committed matrix is the gate. Scrambled order frozen in `archive-config.json`: `F3,F2,G,F1,W` (rank 0.25, naive later-wins fraction 0.7, ordered-not-temporal).
4. **Vigour residual directional statistic** uses centre 0 (later minus earlier residual), not 0.5; implemented in `permutationRank(m, centre)`.
5. **Selection outcome** (screen seeds 901..903, 13 genomes screened, all passed): all three sources kept with five viable bins each (`bootstrap-m3` horizon 80, `bootstrap-200` horizon 298, `bootstrap-div` horizon 80). The first-ranked elite of bin 2 of `bootstrap-m3` is byte-identical to bin 0 of `bootstrap-200` and bin 2 of `bootstrap-div` (flagged `shared-genome`; none of the three sources is wholly shared, so none is dropped). Elite `quality` is saturated near 1.0 for most elites, so ties (broken by cell then born) decide many bins.
6. **Ordering of "ordered" strength**: `ordered` means no directed cycle among confirmed edges and at least 3 confirmed edges (a topological strength order exists).
7. **Review-driven changes before freeze (Sol xhigh freeze review, 8 findings)**: the freeze inventory now includes `stats.ts`, all schema and sim-ref sources, PLAN.md and chain-calibration.json; `--check` verifies freeze provenance (ancestor, inventory, manifest commit); `--replay-check` runs the full saved-evidence and freeze verification before any simulation and refuses without evidence; the planted-chain gate requires every scheduled chain pair informative; viability flags in `archive.json` are recomputed from the recorded counts; test-only seeds 7001, 7002 replace the reserved scoring seeds in pre-freeze tests.

    67	export const keptSources = (fx: ArchiveFixture): SourceFixture[] => fx.sources.filter((s) => s.status === "kept");
    68	export const chainId = (k: number): string => `p${k}`;
    69	export const weakId = (id: string): string => `${id}w`;
    70	const pk = (a: string, b: string): string => `${a}|${b}`;
    71	const MASKS: Dict<number[]> = { m01: [0, 1], m23: [2, 3] };

    73	export interface Plan {
    74	  jobs: JobSpec[];
    75	  /** Per kept source: snapshot ids in time order and the latest id (twin and weakened copy are of it). */
    76	  sources: { short: string; ids: string[] }[];
    77	  starved: [string, string] | null;
    78	  chain: string[];
    79	}
    80	export function planOf(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture): Plan {
    81	  const jobs: JobSpec[] = [];
    82	  const bal = (a: string, b: string, depletion = false): void => {
    83	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const pos of ["P1", "P2"] as const) {
    84	      const s = ARMS.bal[pos];
    85	      jobs.push({ key: `${depletion ? "d" : "c"}|${pk(a, b)}|${depletion ? "" : "bal|"}${pos}|${seed}`, seed, a, b, slotsA: [...s.A], slotsB: [...s.B], depletion });
    86	    }
    87	  };
    88	  const solo = (id: string): void => {
    89	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const m of ["m01", "m23"]) jobs.push({ key: `s|${id}|${m}|${seed}`, seed, a: id, b: null, slotsA: MASKS[m], slotsB: [], depletion: false });
    90	  };
    91	  const sources: Plan["sources"] = [];
    92	  for (const s of keptSources(fx)) {
    93	    const ids = s.snapshots.map((x) => x.id);
    94	    sources.push({ short: s.short, ids });
    95	    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < ids.length; j++) bal(ids[i], ids[j]);
    96	    ids.forEach(solo);
    97	    const L = ids[ids.length - 1];
    98	    bal(L, twinOf(L));
    99	    bal(L, weakId(L));
   100	    solo(weakId(L));
   101	  }
   102	  // calibrated weakened-copy gate and the planted chain (instrument genomes)
   103	  bal("G", "W");
   104	  solo("G"); solo("W");
   105	  const chain = cfg.chain.percents.map((_, k) => chainId(k));
   106	  for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < chain.length; j++) bal(chain[i], chain[j]);
   107	  chain.forEach(solo);
   108	  const first = sources[0];
   109	  const starved: [string, string] | null = first && first.ids.length >= 2 ? [first.ids[0], first.ids[1]] : null;
   110	  if (starved) bal(starved[0], starved[1], true);
   111	  jobs.sort((x, y) => (x.key < y.key ? -1 : x.key > y.key ? 1 : 0));
   112	  const seen = new Set<string>();
   113	  for (const j of jobs) { if (seen.has(j.key)) throw new Error(`duplicate job key ${j.key}`); seen.add(j.key); }
   114	  return { jobs, sources, starved, chain };
   115	}

   124	const obs = (r: RunRec, k: number): Obs => ({ b0A: r.b0[0], b0B: r.b0[1], bA: r.cp[k][0], bB: r.cp[k][1] });
   125	type Index = Map<string, RunRec>;
   126	function blocksFor(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number, kind: "c" | "d" = "c"): BlockPayoff[] {
   127	  return seeds.map((seed) => {
   128	    const mk = (pos: string): RunRec => {
   129	      const r = idx.get(kind === "c" ? `c|${pk(a, b)}|bal|${pos}|${seed}` : `d|${pk(a, b)}|${pos}|${seed}`);
   130	      if (!r) throw new Error(`missing run ${pk(a, b)} ${pos} ${seed}`);
   131	      return r;
   132	    };
   133	    return blockPayoff(seed, [obs(mk("P1"), k), obs(mk("P2"), k)]);
   134	  });
   135	}
   136	/** Mean over seeds of observed payoff minus solo-predicted share; null when no seed is usable. */
   137	function residualOf(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number): { observed: number; expected: number; residual: number; blocks: number } | null {
   138	  const per: { payoff: number; expected: number }[] = [];
   139	  for (const seed of seeds) {
   140	    const blk = blocksFor(idx, a, b, [seed], k)[0];
   141	    if (blk.payoff === null) continue;
   142	    const v = (id: string, m: string): number => (idx.get(`s|${id}|${m}|${seed}`) as RunRec).cp[k][0];
   143	    const e1 = vigourExpected(v(a, "m01"), v(b, "m23")), e2 = vigourExpected(v(a, "m23"), v(b, "m01"));
   144	    if (e1 === null || e2 === null) continue;
   145	    per.push({ payoff: blk.payoff, expected: (e1 + e2) / 2 });
   146	  }
   147	  return per.length ? { observed: mean(per.map((x) => x.payoff)), expected: mean(per.map((x) => x.expected)), residual: mean(per.map((x) => x.payoff - x.expected)), blocks: per.length } : null;
   148	}
   149	
   150	export interface PairRec { a: string; b: string; summary: PairSummary; vigour: { observed: number; expected: number; residual: number; blocks: number } | null }
   151	export interface TournamentSummary {
   152	  ids: string[];
   153	  pairs: PairRec[];
   154	  payoff: Mat;
   155	  resid: Mat;
   156	  edges: boolean[][];
   157	  analysis: Analysis;
   158	  permuted: number[];
   159	  /** Descriptive extras: solo growth at the primary checkpoint per snapshot (mean over masks and seeds) and its step-zero biomass. */
   160	  solo: Dict<{ mean: number; b0: number; aliveAll: boolean }>;
   161	}
   162	/** One tournament over `ids` in time order. */
   163	export function tournament(idx: Index, ids: string[], seeds: number[], k: number): TournamentSummary {
   164	  const n = ids.length;
   165	  const payoff: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
   166	  const resid: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
   167	  const edges = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(false));
   168	  const pairs: PairRec[] = [];
   169	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
   170	    const summary = summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, ids[i], ids[j], seeds, k), COMPLETE_FRACTION.balanced);
   171	    const vigour = residualOf(idx, ids[i], ids[j], seeds, k);
   172	    pairs.push({ a: ids[i], b: ids[j], summary, vigour });
   173	    if (summary.status === "informative" && summary.mean !== null) {
   174	      payoff[i][j] = summary.mean;
   175	      resid[i][j] = vigour ? vigour.residual : null;
   176	    }
   177	    if (summary.edge === "A>B") edges[i][j] = true;
   178	    if (summary.edge === "B>A") edges[j][i] = true;
   179	  }
   180	  const analysis = analyze({ payoff, resid, edges });
   181	  const solo: TournamentSummary["solo"] = {};
   182	  for (const id of ids) {
   183	    const rs = [...idx.values()].filter((r) => r.key.startsWith(`s|${id}|`));
   184	    solo[id] = { mean: mean(rs.map((r) => r.cp[k][0])), b0: mean(rs.map((r) => r.b0[0])), aliveAll: rs.every((r) => !atFloor(r.cp[k][0], r.b0[0])) };
   185	  }
   186	  return { ids, pairs, payoff, resid, edges, analysis, permuted: permutedValues(payoff), solo };
   187	}
   188	
   189	export interface TwinSummary { id: string; labelInvariance: boolean; positionAdvantage: (number | null)[]; signFlipP: number | null; meanAbs: number | null; effectTarget: number; positionOk: boolean }
   190	function twinSummary(idx: Index, id: string, seeds: number[], k: number, target: number): TwinSummary {
   191	  const t = twinOf(id);
   192	  let inv = true;
   193	  const adv: (number | null)[] = [];
   194	  for (const seed of seeds) {
   195	    const r1 = idx.get(`c|${pk(id, t)}|bal|P1|${seed}`) as RunRec, r2 = idx.get(`c|${pk(id, t)}|bal|P2|${seed}`) as RunRec;
   196	    // same genome under two labels: the reciprocal worlds are one physical world, so counts swap exactly
   197	    if (r1.b0[0] !== r2.b0[1] || r1.b0[1] !== r2.b0[0]) inv = false;
   198	    r1.cp.forEach((c, kk) => { const d = r2.cp[kk]; if (c[0] !== d[1] || c[1] !== d[0] || c[2] !== d[3] || c[3] !== d[2]) inv = false; });
   199	    const o = obs(r1, k);
   200	    adv.push(o.bA + o.bB === 0 ? null : o.bA / (o.bA + o.bB) - o.b0A / (o.b0A + o.b0B));
   201	  }
   202	  const ok = adv.filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
   203	  const p = ok.length === adv.length ? signFlipP(ok) : null;
   204	  const meanAbs = ok.length === adv.length && ok.length ? mean(ok.map(Math.abs)) : null;
   205	  return { id, labelInvariance: inv, positionAdvantage: adv, signFlipP: p, meanAbs, effectTarget: target, positionOk: p !== null && p > 0.05 && meanAbs !== null && meanAbs < target };
   206	}
   207	
   208	export interface WeakSummary { a: string; b: string; complete: number; allBelowHalf: boolean; meanPayoffB: number | null; soloAboveFloor: boolean | null; detected: boolean }
   209	function weakSummary(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number, soloCheck: boolean): WeakSummary {
   210	  const s = summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, a, b, seeds, k));
   211	  const wp = s.payoffs.map((x) => 1 - x);
   212	  const alive = soloCheck ? ["m01", "m23"].every((m) => seeds.every((sd) => { const r = idx.get(`s|${b}|${m}|${sd}`) as RunRec; return !atFloor(r.cp[k][0], r.b0[0]); })) : null;
   213	  const allBelowHalf = wp.length > 0 && wp.every((x) => x < 0.5);
   214	  const meanB = wp.length ? mean(wp) : null;
   215	  return { a, b, complete: wp.length, allBelowHalf, meanPayoffB: meanB, soloAboveFloor: alive, detected: allBelowHalf && wp.length >= EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS && meanB !== null && meanB <= G2_MEAN_MAX && alive !== false };
   216	}

   218	export interface SourceSummary {
   219	  short: string; name: string; tournament: TournamentSummary; twin: TwinSummary;
   220	  /** Descriptive: weakened copy of the latest snapshot, with its solo viability. */
   221	  weakCopy: WeakSummary;
   222	  label: TemporalLabel; underlying: TemporalLabel | null;
   223	  flags: { controlInvalid: boolean; insensitiveOrBiased: boolean };
   224	}
   225	export interface Starved { pair: [string, string]; bothBelowFloorEveryRun: boolean; status: string; inventories: { seed: number; pos: string; fracA: number; fracB: number }[] }
   226	export interface Summary {
   227	  checkpoint: number;
   228	  sources: SourceSummary[];
   229	  gw: WeakSummary;
   230	  starved: Starved | null;
   231	  chain: { ids: string[]; percents: number[]; tournament: TournamentSummary; fixture: FixtureResult };
   232	  fixtures: FixtureResult[];
   233	  naiveVersusSealed: { id: string; naive: number; label: TemporalLabel }[];
   234	  global: { runsValid: boolean; starvedOk: boolean; gwDetected: boolean; assayOk: boolean };
   235	  labelCounts: Dict<number>;
   236	}
   237	export const labelsOf = (s: Summary): Dict<string> => Object.fromEntries(s.sources.map((x) => [x.short, x.label]));

   239	export interface FirstRung { ids: string[]; payoff: Mat; edges: boolean[][]; resid: Mat | null; meanPayoff: Dict<number | null> }
   240	/** Full-layout panel matrix from the first rung's committed summary (payoff mirrored, residual antisymmetric). */
   241	export function panelFromFirstRung(p: { summary: { matrix: { ids: string[]; payoff: Mat; edges: boolean[][]; meanPayoff: Dict<number | null> }; vigour: Dict<{ residual: number | null } | null> } }): PanelMatrix {
   242	  const { ids, payoff, edges, meanPayoff } = p.summary.matrix;
   243	  const resid: Mat = ids.map(() => ids.map(() => null));
   244	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p.summary.vigour)) {
   245	    const [a, b] = k.split("|");
   246	    const i = ids.indexOf(a), j = ids.indexOf(b);
   247	    if (v && v.residual !== null) { resid[i][j] = v.residual; resid[j][i] = -v.residual; }
   248	  }
   249	  return { ids, payoff, resid, edges, meanPayoff };
   250	}
   251	
   252	export function summarize(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, runs: RunRec[], panel: PanelMatrix, checkpoints: number[]): Summary {
   253	  const idx: Index = new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
   254	  const plan = planOf(cfg, fx);
   255	  if (plan.jobs.length !== runs.length || plan.jobs.some((j) => !idx.has(j.key))) throw new Error("run records do not match the schedule");
   256	  const k = checkpoints.length - 1;
   257	  const runsValid = validateRuns({ arena: cfg.arena } as never, runs, checkpoints).length === 0;
   258	  const gw = weakSummary(idx, "G", "W", cfg.seeds, k, true);
   259	  let starved: Starved | null = null;
   260	  if (plan.starved) {
   261	    const [a, b] = plan.starved;
   262	    const inventories: Starved["inventories"] = [];
   263	    let all = true;
   264	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const pos of ["P1", "P2"]) {
   265	      const o = obs(idx.get(`d|${pk(a, b)}|${pos}|${seed}`) as RunRec, k);
   266	      inventories.push({ seed, pos, fracA: o.bA / o.b0A, fracB: o.bB / o.b0B });
   267	      if (!(atFloor(o.bA, o.b0A) && atFloor(o.bB, o.b0B))) all = false;
   268	    }
   269	    starved = { pair: [a, b], bothBelowFloorEveryRun: all, status: summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, a, b, cfg.seeds, k, "d")).status, inventories };
   270	  }
   271	  const starvedOk = !plan.starved || (!!starved && starved.bothBelowFloorEveryRun && starved.status === "uninformative");
   272	  const chainT = tournament(idx, plan.chain, cfg.seeds, k);
   273	  const chainFx = chainFixture(chainT.analysis, cfg.rDirMax, plan.chain);
   274	  const fixtures = [...runFixtures({ rDirMax: cfg.rDirMax, scrambled: cfg.scrambled, panel }), chainFx];
   275	  const byId = (id: string): FixtureResult => fixtures.find((f) => f.id === id) as FixtureResult;
   276	  const assayOk = chainFx.pass && byId("strength-scrambled").pass && byId("vigour-only").pass && byId("cycle-fools-naive").pass && byId("long-cycle-fools-naive").pass && byId("strength-aligned").pass;
   277	  const sources: SourceSummary[] = plan.sources.map((ps) => {
   278	    const name = keptSources(fx).find((s) => s.short === ps.short)!.name;
   279	    const t = tournament(idx, ps.ids, cfg.seeds, k);
   280	    const L = ps.ids[ps.ids.length - 1];
   281	    const twin = twinSummary(idx, L, cfg.seeds, k, cfg.effectTarget);
   282	    const weakCopy = weakSummary(idx, L, weakId(L), cfg.seeds, k, true);
   283	    const controlInvalid = !runsValid || !twin.labelInvariance || !starvedOk;
   284	    const insensitiveOrBiased = !twin.positionOk || !gw.detected;
   285	    const { label, underlying } = temporalLabel({
   286	      controlInvalid, insensitiveOrBiased, assayFailed: !assayOk, scheduled: t.analysis.scheduled, informative: t.analysis.informative,
   287	      rDir: t.analysis.rank.rDir, rDirResid: t.analysis.residRank?.rDir ?? null, ordered: t.analysis.ordered, rDirMax: cfg.rDirMax,
   288	    });
   289	    return { short: ps.short, name, tournament: t, twin, weakCopy, label, underlying, flags: { controlInvalid, insensitiveOrBiased } };
   290	  });
   291	  const labelCounts: Dict<number> = Object.fromEntries(TEMPORAL_LABELS.map((l) => [l, 0]));
   292	  for (const s of sources) labelCounts[s.label]++;
   293	  const naiveVersusSealed = [
   294	    ...fixtures.map((f) => ({ id: f.id, naive: f.naive.fraction, label: f.label })),
   295	    ...sources.map((s) => ({ id: `source:${s.short}`, naive: s.tournament.analysis.naive.fraction, label: s.label })),
   296	  ];
   297	  return {
   298	    checkpoint: checkpoints[k], sources, gw, starved, chain: { ids: plan.chain, percents: cfg.chain.percents, tournament: chainT, fixture: chainFx }, fixtures, naiveVersusSealed,
   299	    global: { runsValid, starvedOk, gwDetected: gw.detected, assayOk }, labelCounts,
   300	  };
   301	}

   305	export const LIMITS = [
   306	  "descriptive tournament of related surviving elites from three related bootstrap searches (all record search seed 1); no confirmatory claim, no pooled rank or p",
   307	  "archive time is the bootstrap batch index (`born`), not ecological time; the archive holds final elites, one per MAP-Elites cell, not snapshots captured during the run",
   308	  "the permutation rank r_dir is a rank against relabelled times in a genealogically related, non-exchangeable set; it is never called significant",
   309	  "the search optimised survival, recovery and light dependence, not arena competition; the arena is a 32x32 tile with gradient light, amplitude 200 and mu/sigma 154/24, unlike the search worlds (uniform light, amplitude 160, 60/20)",
   310	  "payoff is biomass share at step 300 in a small CPU arena with one chemistry; balanced arm only; rare starts are not run because they are biased against the rare side even for identical genomes",
   311	  "'nothing-beyond-null' means none detected at this resolution, never that none exists; ten seed blocks give wide intervals",
   312	  "the weakened copy of an archive genome is descriptive: the edit scales one generalist-specific weight and can leave a negative-weight genome less impaired",
   313	  "the cycle and vigour-only fixtures are synthetic; strength-not-time is a pure-matrix fixture on the first rung's committed panel matrix, with no in-world rerun",
   314	  "no fixture or control reaches directional-rank: strength-aligned and the planted chain land on directional-vigour-only, so the top label has no positive control and a real directional-rank would be unvalidated",
   315	  "no physics, schema, preset or RULE_VERSION change; no paid calls; not on the public site",
   316	];

   318	export interface Payload {
   319	  kind: "historical-opponents-archive-evidence"; version: 1;
   320	  statements: readonly string[]; note: string;
   321	  config: ArchiveConfig;
   322	  archiveSha256: string;
   323	  firstRungEvidenceSha256: string;
   324	  panel: PanelMatrix;
   325	  hex: Dict<string>;
   326	  constants: { floorDiv: number; edgeMinBlocks: number; edgeMinOffset: number; familySize: number; g2MeanMax: number; completeFraction: number; checkpoints: number[]; plant: Dict<number>; ruleVersion: number };
   327	  schedule: ScheduleCounts;
   328	  runs: RunRec[];
   329	  summary: Summary;
   330	  verdict: Dict<string>;
   331	  limits: string[];
   332	  integritySha256?: string;
   333	}
   334	export const expectedConstants = (checkpoints: number[], plant: Dict<number>, ruleVersion: number): Payload["constants"] => ({
   335	  floorDiv: FLOOR_DIV, edgeMinBlocks: EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS, edgeMinOffset: EDGE_MIN_OFFSET, familySize: FAMILY, g2MeanMax: G2_MEAN_MAX, completeFraction: COMPLETE_FRACTION.balanced, checkpoints, plant, ruleVersion,
   336	});
   337	
   338	export function buildPayload(
   339	  cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, runs: RunRec[], panel: PanelMatrix, hex: Dict<string>, archiveSha256: string, firstRungEvidenceSha256: string,
   340	  checkpoints: number[], plant: Dict<number>, ruleVersion: number,
   341	): Payload {
   342	  const sorted = [...runs].sort((x, y) => (x.key < y.key ? -1 : 1));
   343	  const bad = validateRuns({ arena: cfg.arena } as never, sorted, checkpoints);
   344	  if (bad.length) throw new Error(`invalid run records: ${bad.join("; ")}`);
   345	  const summary = summarize(cfg, fx, sorted, panel, checkpoints);
   346	  return {
   347	    kind: "historical-opponents-archive-evidence", version: 1, statements: STATEMENTS, note: SALT_NOTE, config: cfg, archiveSha256, firstRungEvidenceSha256, panel, hex,
   348	    constants: expectedConstants(checkpoints, plant, ruleVersion), schedule: scheduleCounts(planOf(cfg, fx).jobs), runs: sorted, summary, verdict: labelsOf(summary), limits: LIMITS,
   349	  };
   350	}
   351	export function sealPayload(p: Payload): Payload {
   352	  const { integritySha256: _d, ...rest } = p;
   353	  return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) };
   354	}
   355	export function verifySeal(p: Payload): boolean {
   356	  const { integritySha256, ...rest } = p;
   357	  return typeof integritySha256 === "string" && integritySha256 === sha256(canonical(rest));
   358	}
   359	
   360	/** Recompute every summary and verdict from the raw runs alone and report differences from what is stored. */
   361	export function recompute(p: Payload, fx: ArchiveFixture): string[] {
   362	  const problems: string[] = [];
   363	  try {
   364	    const bad = validateRuns({ arena: p.config.arena } as never, p.runs, p.constants.checkpoints);
   365	    if (bad.length) return bad.map((m) => `invalid run record: ${m}`);
   366	    const summary = summarize(p.config, fx, p.runs, p.panel, p.constants.checkpoints);
   367	    const norm = (x: unknown): string => canonical(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)));
   368	    if (norm(summary) !== norm(p.summary)) problems.push("stored summary differs from the summary recomputed from the raw runs");
   369	    if (norm(labelsOf(summary)) !== norm(p.verdict)) problems.push(`stored verdict ${JSON.stringify(p.verdict)} differs from recomputed ${JSON.stringify(labelsOf(summary))}`);
   370	    if (norm(p.limits) !== norm(LIMITS)) problems.push("stored limits differ from the current limits");
   371	    if (norm(scheduleCounts(planOf(p.config, fx).jobs)) !== norm(p.schedule)) problems.push("stored schedule counts differ");
   372	  } catch (e) {
   373	    problems.push(`recompute failed: ${(e as Error).message}`);
   374	  }
   375	  return problems;
   376	}

    41	/** Frozen inventory: every scientific file. Compared byte for byte with the freeze commit. */
    42	export function frozenFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
    43	  const ls = (d: string, ext: string) => (existsSync(root + d) ? readdirSync(root + d).filter((f) => f.endsWith(ext)).sort().map((f) => d + f) : []);
    44	  return [
    45	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/archive-tournament/", ".ts"),
    46	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/opponents/", ".ts"),
    47	    ...ls("packages/schema/src/", ".ts"),
    48	    ...ls("packages/sim-ref/src/", ".ts"),
    49	    "packages/metrics/src/stats.ts",
    50	    "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/PLAN.md", "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/chain-calibration.json",
    51	    "tools/opponents/archive.ts", "tools/opponents/archive-config.json", "tools/opponents/assay.ts", "tools/opponents/boot.ts", "tools/opponents/worker.ts", "tools/opponents/config.json",
    52	    "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/archive.json", "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json",
    53	  ];
    54	}
    55	const OUTCOME_FILES = ["docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/evidence.json", "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/manifest.json"];
    56	const git = (root: string, args: string[]): string => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }).trim();
    57	const fileAt = (root: string, commit: string, path: string): Buffer | null => { try { return execFileSync("git", ["show", `${commit}:${path}`], { cwd: root, maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }); } catch { return null; } };
    58	
    59	/** First commit that added the frozen config, unless `commit` is given. */
    60	export function freezeCommit(root = REPO, configPath = "tools/opponents/archive-config.json"): string | null {
    61	  const out = git(root, ["log", "--diff-filter=A", "--format=%H", "--", configPath]);
    62	  const lines = out.split("\n").filter(Boolean);
    63	  return lines.length ? lines[lines.length - 1] : null;
    64	}
    65	export interface FreezeResult { problems: string[]; commit: string | null; inventory: Record<string, string> }
    66	/** Verifies the freeze: ancestor of HEAD, every inventoried file byte-identical to the commit, no outcome files in it, manifest agrees. */
    67	export function freezeCheck(root = REPO, files: string[] = frozenFiles(root), commit?: string, manifestPath = MANIFEST): FreezeResult {
    68	  const problems: string[] = [];
    69	  const c = commit ?? freezeCommit(root);
    70	  if (!c) return { problems: ["no freeze commit: archive-config.json was never committed"], commit: null, inventory: {} };
    71	  try { git(root, ["merge-base", "--is-ancestor", c, "HEAD"]); } catch { problems.push(`freeze commit ${c} is not an ancestor of HEAD`); }
    72	  const tree = git(root, ["ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", c]).split("\n");
    73	  for (const o of OUTCOME_FILES) if (tree.includes(o)) problems.push(`outcome file ${o} is in the freeze commit`);
    74	  const inventory: Record<string, string> = {};
    75	  for (const f of files) {
    76	    const then = fileAt(root, c, f);
    77	    if (!then) { problems.push(`frozen file ${f} is not in the freeze commit`); continue; }
    78	    inventory[f] = sha256(then);
    79	    if (!existsSync(root + f)) problems.push(`frozen file ${f} is missing from the working tree`);
    80	    else if (sha256(readFileSync(root + f)) !== inventory[f]) problems.push(`frozen file ${f} changed after the freeze`);
    81	  }
    82	  if (existsSync(manifestPath)) {
    83	    const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
    84	    if (m.freeze?.commit !== c) problems.push("manifest freeze commit differs from the freeze commit");
    85	    else if (canonical(m.freeze.inventory) !== canonical(inventory)) problems.push("manifest freeze inventory differs from the freeze commit");
    86	  }
    87	  return { problems, commit: c, inventory };
    88	}

    92	export function hexMap(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture): Record<string, string> {
    93	  const P = buildPanel(cfg.weakenPercent);
    94	  const hex: Record<string, string> = { G: P.hex.G, W: P.hex.W };
    95	  cfg.chain.percents.forEach((p, k) => { hex[`p${k}`] = genomeHex(p === 0 ? P.genomes.G : weaken(P.genomes.G, p)); });
    96	  for (const s of keptSources(fx)) {
    97	    for (const sn of s.snapshots) hex[sn.id] = sn.hex;
    98	    const L = s.snapshots[s.snapshots.length - 1];
    99	    hex[weakId(L.id)] = genomeHex(weaken(genomeFromHex(L.hex), cfg.weakenPercent));
   100	  }
   101	  return hex;
   102	}
   103	/** First rung's runJobs reads cfg.arena and cfg.depletion.override only. */
   104	const runnerCfg = (cfg: ArchiveConfig) => ({ arena: cfg.arena, depletion: { override: cfg.depletion, pair: ["", ""] } }) as never;
   105	
   106	export async function generate(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, nWorkers: number, onlyKeys?: Set<string>): Promise<{ runs: RunRec[]; hex: Record<string, string> }> {
   107	  const hex = hexMap(cfg, fx);
   108	  const jobs = planOf(cfg, fx).jobs.filter((j) => !onlyKeys || onlyKeys.has(j.key));
   109	  const runs = await runJobs(runnerCfg(cfg), jobs, hex, nWorkers);
   110	  return { runs, hex };
   111	}
   112	const arenaParams = (cfg: ArchiveConfig, depletion: boolean) => ({ ...cfg.arena, override: depletion ? cfg.depletion : undefined }) as never;

   114	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
   115	export const loadFirstRung = () => panelFromFirstRung(JSON.parse(readFileSync(FIRST_RUNG_EVIDENCE, "utf8")));
   116	
   117	export async function generatePayload(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, archiveText: string, nWorkers: number): Promise<Payload> {
   118	  const { runs, hex } = await generate(cfg, fx, nWorkers);
   119	  const panel = loadFirstRung();
   120	  return sealPayload(buildPayload(cfg, fx, runs, panel, hex, sha256(archiveText), sha256(readFileSync(FIRST_RUNG_EVIDENCE)), checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION));
   121	}

   125	const encGenomeHex = (g: { mu: number; sigma: number; motGain: number; weights: number[]; rings?: [number, number, number] }): string =>
   126	  genomeHex({ mu: g.mu, sigma: g.sigma, motGain: g.motGain, weights: Int8Array.from(g.weights), ...(g.rings ? { rings: g.rings } : {}) });
   127	
   128	/** Decidable compatibility: the genome decodes (round trip) and the arena world built from it validates. */
   129	export function compat(cfg: ArchiveConfig, hex: string): string[] {
   130	  const problems: string[] = [];
   131	  try {
   132	    if (genomeHex(genomeFromHex(hex)) !== hex) problems.push("hex does not round-trip");
   133	    const job = { key: "compat", seed: cfg.screen.seeds[0], hexA: hex, hexB: null, slotsA: [0, 1], slotsB: [] };
   134	    const p = { ...cfg.arena };
   135	    problems.push(...validateConfig(contestConfig(job.seed, p)).map((m: string) => `config: ${m}`));
   136	    problems.push(...validateState(buildContestWorld(job, p)).map((m: string) => `state: ${m}`));
   137	  } catch (e) { problems.push((e as Error).message); }
   138	  return problems;
   139	}

   141	export const TRANSFER_CONDITIONS = [
   142	  "search worlds: 64x64 tile, uniform light (base 40, amplitude 160), mu/sigma 60/20, kernel radius 9; arena: 32x32 tile, gradient light, amplitude 200, mu/sigma 154/24 (the first rung's arena)",
   143	  "search score: survival, recovery after a 30% lesion and light dependence over 7000 steps; arena payoff: biomass share at step 300 against another label, mutation off",
   144	  "bootstrap-200 records a legacy resume with exact:false (resumed from its elites and gate passers, not byte-exact)",
   145	];
   146	
   147	export async function selectArchive(cfg: ArchiveConfig, runsDir: string, nWorkers: number): Promise<ArchiveFixture> {
   148	  const raw = cfg.sources.map((s) => {
   149	    const file = `${runsDir}/${s.name}/archive.json`;
   150	    const bytes = readFileSync(file);
   151	    const a = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));
   152	    const elites: Elite[] = a.elites.map((e: { cell: [number, number]; born: number; quality: number; genome: never }) => ({ cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: encGenomeHex(e.genome) }));
   153	    return { s, file, sha: sha256(bytes), a, elites };
   154	  });
   155	  const screens = new Map<string, { viable: boolean; b0: number[]; b300: number[] }>();
   156	  const selections = () => raw.map((r) => {
   157	    const need: string[] = [];
   158	    const sel = selectSnapshots(r.elites, cfg.bins, (e) => {
   159	      const sc = screens.get(e.hex);
   160	      if (sc) return sc.viable;
   161	      need.push(e.hex);
   162	      return true; // provisional: stops this bin until screened
   163	    });
   164	    return { sel, need };
   165	  });
   166	  for (;;) {
   167	    const sels = selections();
   168	    const need = [...new Set(sels.flatMap((x) => x.need))];
   169	    if (!need.length) break;
   170	    const hex: Record<string, string> = {};
   171	    const jobs: JobSpec[] = [];
   172	    need.forEach((h, i) => {
   173	      hex[`g${i}`] = h;
   174	      for (const seed of cfg.screen.seeds) jobs.push({ key: `s|g${i}|m01|${seed}`, seed, a: `g${i}`, b: null, slotsA: [0, 1], slotsB: [], depletion: false });
   175	    });
   176	    const runs = await runJobs(runnerCfg(cfg), jobs, hex, nWorkers);
   177	    const byKey = new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
   178	    need.forEach((h, i) => {
   179	      const rs = cfg.screen.seeds.map((seed) => byKey.get(`s|g${i}|m01|${seed}`) as RunRec);
   180	      const b0 = rs.map((r) => r.b0[0]), b300 = rs.map((r) => r.cp[r.cp.length - 1][0]);
   181	      screens.set(h, { viable: rs.every((r) => !atFloor(r.cp[r.cp.length - 1][0], r.b0[0])), b0, b300 });
   182	    });
   183	    console.error(`screened ${need.length} genomes (${screens.size} total)`);
   184	  }
   185	  const final = selections();
   186	  const sources: SourceFixture[] = [];
   187	  const seenHex = new Map<string, string>();
   188	  raw.forEach((r, si) => {
   189	    const { sel } = final[si];
   190	    const snaps: SnapshotRec[] = sel.bins.filter((b) => b.pick).map((b) => {
   191	      const e = b.pick as Elite;
   192	      return { id: `${r.s.short}:${b.bin}`, bin: b.bin, cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: e.hex, sharedWith: [] };
   193	    });
   194	    for (const sn of snaps) {
   195	      const prior = seenHex.get(sn.hex);
   196	      if (prior) sn.sharedWith.push(prior);
   197	    }
   198	    snaps.forEach((sn) => { if (!seenHex.has(sn.hex)) seenHex.set(sn.hex, sn.id); });
   199	    const problems = snaps.flatMap((sn) => compat(cfg, sn.hex).map((m) => `${sn.id}: ${m}`));
   200	    let status: SourceFixture["status"] = "kept", dropReason: string | null = null;
   201	    if (viableBins(sel.bins) < cfg.minViableBins) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = `fewer than ${cfg.minViableBins} viable bins (${viableBins(sel.bins)})`; }
   202	    else if (si > 0 && snaps.every((sn) => sn.sharedWith.length > 0)) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = "every snapshot is byte-identical to a snapshot of an earlier source"; }
   203	    else if (problems.length) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = `incompatible with the arena: ${problems[0]}`; }
   204	    const resumes = r.a.resumes as { from: number; exact: boolean }[] | undefined;
   205	    sources.push({
   206	      name: r.s.name, short: r.s.short, file: `runs/${r.s.name}/archive.json`, fileSha256: r.sha, evaluated: r.a.evaluated, elites: r.elites.length, horizon: sel.horizon,
   207	      resumeExact: resumes ? resumes.every((x) => x.exact) : null,
   208	      bins: sel.bins.map((b) => ({ bin: b.bin, lo: b.lo, hi: b.hi, status: b.status, candidates: b.candidates, screened: b.screened, snapshot: b.pick ? `${r.s.short}:${b.bin}` : null })),
   209	      snapshots: snaps, status, dropReason, compat: { ok: problems.length === 0, problems },
   210	      screens: [...new Set(sel.bins.flatMap((b) => (b.pick ? [b.pick.hex] : [])))].map((h) => ({ hex: h, ...(screens.get(h) as { viable: boolean; b0: number[]; b300: number[] }) })),
   211	    });
   212	  });
   213	  return { kind: "historical-opponents-archive-fixture", version: 1, transferConditions: TRANSFER_CONDITIONS, screenSeeds: cfg.screen.seeds, sources };
   214	}

   218	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
   219	  const ls = (d: string) => (existsSync(root + d) ? readdirSync(root + d).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f) : []);
   220	  return [
   221	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/archive-tournament/"), ...ls("packages/metrics/src/opponents/"), ...ls("packages/schema/src/"), ...ls("packages/sim-ref/src/"),
   222	    "packages/metrics/src/stats.ts", "tools/opponents/archive.ts", "tools/opponents/archive-config.json", "tools/opponents/assay.ts", "tools/opponents/worker.ts", "tools/opponents/boot.ts", "tools/opponents/config.json",
   223	  ];
   224	}
   225	const sourceHashes = (root = REPO): Record<string, string> => Object.fromEntries(sourceFiles(root).map((f) => [f, sha256(readFileSync(root + f))]));
   226	
   227	export function makeManifest(payload: Payload, evidenceText: string, freeze: FreezeResult, cpuSeconds: number | null): Record<string, unknown> {
   228	  return {
   229	    kind: "historical-opponents-archive-manifest",
   230	    payloadFileSha256: sha256(evidenceText),
   231	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
   232	    archiveSha256: payload.archiveSha256,
   233	    config: payload.config,
   234	    constants: payload.constants,
   235	    schedule: payload.schedule,
   236	    verdict: payload.verdict,
   237	    freeze: { commit: freeze.commit, inventory: freeze.inventory },
   238	    sources: sourceHashes(),
   239	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), cpuSeconds },
   240	  };
   241	}
   242	
   243	export const expectedHex = (cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture) => hexMap(cfg, fx);
   244	
   245	/** Compare the archive fixture with the source files when `runsDir` is given; otherwise only internal consistency. Returns problems and a skip note. */
   246	export function checkFixture(fx: ArchiveFixture, cfg: ArchiveConfig, runsDir: string | null): { problems: string[]; note: string | null } {
   247	  const problems: string[] = [];
   248	  for (const s of fx.sources) {
   249	    for (const sc of s.screens) {
   250	      const ints = (v: unknown): v is number[] => Array.isArray(v) && v.length === cfg.screen.seeds.length && v.every((x) => Number.isInteger(x) && x >= 0);
   251	      if (!ints(sc.b0) || !ints(sc.b300) || sc.b0.some((x) => x <= 0)) problems.push(`${s.short}: screen record for ${sc.hex.slice(0, 12)} has malformed counts`);
   252	      else if (sc.viable !== sc.b300.every((b, i) => !atFloor(b, sc.b0[i]))) problems.push(`${s.short}: screen record for ${sc.hex.slice(0, 12)} has a viable flag that contradicts its counts`);
   253	    }
   254	    const viableHex = new Set(s.screens.filter((x) => x.viable).map((x) => x.hex));
   255	    for (const sn of s.snapshots) {
   256	      if (!viableHex.has(sn.hex)) problems.push(`${sn.id}: snapshot has no passing viability screen`);
   257	      if (compat(cfg, sn.hex).length) problems.push(`${sn.id}: snapshot is not arena-compatible`);
   258	    }
   259	  }
   260	  if (canonical(fx.screenSeeds) !== canonical(cfg.screen.seeds)) problems.push("fixture screen seeds differ from the config");
   261	  if (!runsDir) return { problems, note: "source-hash check skipped: RUNS is not set (runs/ exists only in the main checkout)" };
   262	  for (const s of fx.sources) {
   263	    const file = `${runsDir}/${s.name}/archive.json`;
   264	    if (!existsSync(file)) { problems.push(`${s.name}: source file missing under RUNS`); continue; }
   265	    const bytes = readFileSync(file);
   266	    if (sha256(bytes) !== s.fileSha256) { problems.push(`${s.name}: source file SHA-256 differs from the fixture`); continue; }
   267	    const a = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));
   268	    const elites: Elite[] = a.elites.map((e: { cell: [number, number]; born: number; quality: number; genome: never }) => ({ cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: encGenomeHex(e.genome) }));
   269	    const screened = new Map(s.screens.map((x) => [x.hex, x.viable]));
   270	    const sel = selectSnapshots(elites, cfg.bins, (e) => screened.get(e.hex) ?? false);
   271	    if (sel.horizon !== s.horizon) problems.push(`${s.name}: horizon differs`);
   272	    for (const b of sel.bins) {
   273	      const rec = s.bins[b.bin];
   274	      const want = b.pick ? `${s.short}:${b.bin}` : null;
   275	      if (rec.snapshot !== want) problems.push(`${s.name}: bin ${b.bin} selection differs from the recomputed one`);
   276	      if (b.pick && s.snapshots.find((x) => x.id === want)?.hex !== b.pick.hex) problems.push(`${s.name}: bin ${b.bin} genome differs`);
   277	    }
   278	  }
   279	  return { problems, note: null };
   280	}
   281	
   282	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. */
   283	export function checkSaved(o: { evidencePath?: string; manifestPath?: string; archivePath?: string; configPath?: string; root?: string; runsDir?: string | null } = {}): string[] {
   284	  const evidencePath = o.evidencePath ?? EVIDENCE, manifestPath = o.manifestPath ?? MANIFEST, archivePath = o.archivePath ?? ARCHIVE, root = o.root ?? REPO;
   285	  const problems: string[] = [];
   286	  if (!existsSync(evidencePath) || !existsSync(manifestPath)) return ["evidence.json or manifest.json is missing"];
   287	  const text = readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8");
   288	  const payload = JSON.parse(text) as Payload;
   289	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
   290	  const archiveText = readFileSync(archivePath, "utf8");
   291	  const fx = JSON.parse(archiveText) as ArchiveFixture;
   292	  const cfg: ArchiveConfig = JSON.parse(readFileSync(o.configPath ?? CONFIG_PATH, "utf8"));
   293	  if (!verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
   294	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== sha256(text)) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
   295	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
   296	  if (manifest.archiveSha256 !== sha256(archiveText) || payload.archiveSha256 !== sha256(archiveText)) problems.push("archive.json changed since the evidence was written");
   297	  if (canonical(manifest.config) !== canonical(payload.config)) problems.push("manifest config differs from the payload");
   298	  if (canonical(manifest.verdict) !== canonical(payload.verdict)) problems.push("manifest verdict differs from the payload");
   299	  if (canonical(payload.config) !== canonical(cfg)) problems.push("payload config differs from the frozen archive-config.json");
   300	  if (JSON.stringify(payload.statements) !== JSON.stringify(STATEMENTS)) problems.push("statements differ");
   301	  if (payload.note !== SALT_NOTE) problems.push("payload note differs");
   302	  if (payload.kind !== "historical-opponents-archive-evidence" || payload.version !== 1) problems.push("payload kind or version differs");
   303	  if (canonical(payload.limits) !== canonical(LIMITS)) problems.push("payload limits differ");
   304	  if (payload.firstRungEvidenceSha256 !== sha256(readFileSync(root + "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json"))) problems.push("first rung evidence.json changed");
   305	  const listed = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
   306	  for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!listed.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
   307	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>)) {
   308	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
   309	    else if (sha256(readFileSync(root + file)) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
   310	  }
   311	  const cmp = (name: string, want: unknown, got: unknown) => { if (canonical(want) !== canonical(got ?? null)) problems.push(`${name} differs from the value reconstructed from the frozen inputs`); };
   312	  cmp("payload hex map", hexMap(cfg, fx), payload.hex);
   313	  cmp("payload panel", loadFirstRungAt(root), payload.panel);
   314	  cmp("payload constants", expectedConstants(checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION), payload.constants);
   315	  cmp("payload schedule counts", scheduleCounts(planOf(cfg, fx).jobs), payload.schedule);
   316	  cmp("manifest constants", payload.constants, manifest.constants);
   317	  cmp("manifest schedule", payload.schedule, manifest.schedule);
   318	  if (root === REPO) problems.push(...freezeCheck(REPO, undefined, undefined, manifestPath).problems);
   319	  else if (!manifest.freeze?.commit || !/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(manifest.freeze.commit)) problems.push("manifest has no valid freeze commit");
   320	  const fixture = checkFixture(fx, cfg, o.runsDir === undefined ? (process.env.RUNS ?? null) : o.runsDir);
   321	  problems.push(...fixture.problems);
   322	  problems.push(...recompute(payload, fx));
   323	  problems.push(...checkStepZero(payload, fx));
   324	  return problems;
   325	}
   326	const loadFirstRungAt = (root: string) => panelFromFirstRung(JSON.parse(readFileSync(root + "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json", "utf8")));
   327	
   328	/** Rebuild every run's step-zero world (no simulation step) and compare the stored inventories with it. */
   329	export function checkStepZero(payload: Payload, fx: ArchiveFixture): string[] {
   330	  const out: string[] = [];
   331	  const stored = new Map(payload.runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
   332	  for (const j of planOf(payload.config, fx).jobs) {
   333	    const r = stored.get(j.key);
   334	    if (!r) continue;
   335	    const job = { key: j.key, seed: j.seed, hexA: payload.hex[j.a.replace(/'$/, "")], hexB: j.b === null ? null : payload.hex[j.b.replace(/'$/, "")], slotsA: j.slotsA, slotsB: j.slotsB };
   336	    const p = arenaParams(payload.config, j.depletion);
   337	    const z = stepZero(buildContestWorld(job, p), job, p);
   338	    if (r.b0[0] !== z.b0A || r.b0[1] !== z.b0B || r.e0[0] !== z.e0A || r.e0[1] !== z.e0B) { out.push(`${j.key}: stored step-zero inventories differ from the rebuilt world`); if (out.length >= 10) break; }
   339	  }
   340	  return out;
   341	}
   342	
   343	/** Deterministic sample of jobs: FNV-1a of the key modulo 100 below `pct`. */
   344	export const sampled = (key: string, pct: number): boolean => { let h = 2166136261; for (const c of key) { h ^= c.charCodeAt(0); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; } return h % 100 < pct; };
   345	
   346	export async function replayCheck(nWorkers: number, pct = 100): Promise<string[]> {
   347	  // saved evidence, seal, manifest and freeze provenance are verified first, in full, before any simulation is spent
   348	  if (!existsSync(EVIDENCE) || !existsSync(MANIFEST)) return ["evidence.json or manifest.json is missing: nothing to replay (and no scoring seed is spent)"];
   349	  const pre = checkSaved();
   350	  if (pre.length) return pre;
   351	  const archiveText = readFileSync(ARCHIVE, "utf8");
   352	  const fx = JSON.parse(archiveText) as ArchiveFixture;
   353	  if (pct >= 100) {
   354	    const fresh = serialize(await generatePayload(CONFIG, fx, archiveText, nWorkers));
   355	    return fresh === readFileSync(EVIDENCE, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
   356	  }
   357	  const saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(EVIDENCE, "utf8")) as Payload;
   358	  const keys = new Set(planOf(CONFIG, fx).jobs.map((j) => j.key).filter((k) => sampled(k, pct)));
   359	  const { runs } = await generate(CONFIG, fx, nWorkers, keys);
   360	  const byKey = new Map(saved.runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
   361	  const bad = runs.filter((r) => canonical(r) !== canonical(byKey.get(r.key)));
   362	  console.log(`replayed ${runs.length} of ${saved.runs.length} jobs (${pct}% deterministic sample)`);
   363	  return bad.length ? [`${bad.length} replayed run records differ from evidence.json (first ${bad[0].key})`] : [];
   364	}

   368	/** Tiny config: two seeds, 60 steps, a four-snapshot source of weakened generalists, a two-level chain. */
   369	export function mini(): { cfg: ArchiveConfig; fx: ArchiveFixture } {
   370	  const P = buildPanel(50);
   371	  const hexes = [60, 40, 20, 0].map((p) => genomeHex(p === 0 ? P.genomes.G : weaken(P.genomes.G, p)));
   372	  const cfg: ArchiveConfig = { ...CONFIG, arena: { tile: 32, radius: 3, steps: 60 }, seeds: [7001, 7002], chain: { percents: [60, 40, 20, 0] }, effectTarget: 0.1 };
   373	  const snaps: SnapshotRec[] = hexes.map((h, i) => ({ id: `mm:${i}`, bin: i, cell: [i, 0] as [number, number], born: i, quality: 1, hex: h, sharedWith: [] }));
   374	  const src: SourceFixture = {
   375	    name: "mini", short: "mm", file: "mini", fileSha256: "0".repeat(64), evaluated: 0, elites: 4, horizon: 4, resumeExact: null,
   376	    bins: snaps.map((s) => ({ bin: s.bin, lo: s.bin, hi: s.bin + 1, status: "viable" as const, candidates: 1, screened: 1, snapshot: s.id })), snapshots: snaps, status: "kept", dropReason: null,
   377	    compat: { ok: true, problems: [] }, screens: hexes.map((hex) => ({ hex, viable: true, b0: [], b300: [] })),
   378	  };
   379	  return { cfg, fx: { kind: "historical-opponents-archive-fixture", version: 1, transferConditions: [], screenSeeds: cfg.screen.seeds, sources: [src] } };
   380	}

   383	
   384	const arg = (name: string, dflt: string): string => { const i = process.argv.indexOf(name); return i >= 0 && process.argv[i + 1] ? process.argv[i + 1] : dflt; };
   385	function startCpuGuard(): () => number {
   386	  const t = setInterval(() => { const u = process.cpuUsage(); if ((u.user + u.system) / 1e6 > CPU_CAP_SECONDS) { console.error(`aborting: cumulative CPU exceeded ${CPU_CAP_SECONDS} s`); process.exit(3); } }, 5000);
   387	  t.unref();
   388	  return () => { const u = process.cpuUsage(); return Math.round((u.user + u.system) / 1e6); };
   389	}
   390	
   391	async function main(): Promise<void> {
   392	  const mode = process.argv[2];
   393	  const nWorkers = Number(arg("--jobs", "4"));
   394	  const cpu = startCpuGuard();
   395	  const archiveText = existsSync(ARCHIVE) ? readFileSync(ARCHIVE, "utf8") : "";
   396	  if (mode === "--select") {
   397	    const runsDir = process.env.RUNS;
   398	    if (!runsDir) throw new Error("set RUNS to the main checkout's runs/ directory");
   399	    if (existsSync(ARCHIVE) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("archive.json exists; pass --force to rebuild deliberately");
   400	    const fx = await selectArchive(CONFIG, runsDir, nWorkers);
   401	    writeFileSync(ARCHIVE, serialize(fx));
   402	    for (const s of fx.sources) console.log(`${s.name}: ${s.status}${s.dropReason ? ` (${s.dropReason})` : ""} bins ${s.bins.map((b) => (b.snapshot ? "V" : "-")).join("")} horizon ${s.horizon}`);
   403	    console.log(`cpu ${cpu()} s`);
   404	  } else if (mode === "--freeze-check") {
   405	    const r = freezeCheck();
   406	    if (r.problems.length) { console.error(r.problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   407	    console.log(`--freeze-check ok (freeze commit ${r.commit}, ${Object.keys(r.inventory).length} frozen files)`);
   408	  } else if (mode === "--write") {
   409	    if (existsSync(EVIDENCE) && !process.argv.includes("--force")) throw new Error("evidence.json exists; files are written once. Pass --force to rewrite deliberately.");
   410	    const fr = freezeCheck();
   411	    if (fr.problems.length) throw new Error(`refusing to score before a valid freeze:\n${fr.problems.join("\n")}`);
   412	    const fx = JSON.parse(archiveText) as ArchiveFixture;
   413	    const payload = await generatePayload(CONFIG, fx, archiveText, nWorkers);
   414	    const text = serialize(payload);
   415	    const manifestText = serialize(makeManifest(payload, text, fr, cpu()));
   416	    writeFileSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, text); writeFileSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, manifestText);
   417	    renameSync(`${EVIDENCE}.tmp`, EVIDENCE); renameSync(`${MANIFEST}.tmp`, MANIFEST);
   418	    console.log(`wrote evidence.json (${text.length} bytes) verdict ${JSON.stringify(payload.verdict)} seal ${payload.integritySha256} cpu ${cpu()} s`);
   419	  } else if (mode === "--check") {
   420	    const problems = checkSaved();
   421	    const note = checkFixture(JSON.parse(archiveText), CONFIG, process.env.RUNS ?? null).note;
   422	    if (note) console.log(note);
   423	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   424	    console.log("--check ok");
   425	  } else if (mode === "--replay-check") {
   426	    const problems = await replayCheck(nWorkers, Number(arg("--sample", "100")));
   427	    console.log(`cpu ${cpu()} s`);
   428	    if (problems.length) { console.error(problems.join("\n")); process.exit(1); }
   429	    console.log("--replay-check ok");
   430	  } else if (mode === "--calibrate-chain") {
   431	    const percents = arg("--percents", "70,45,20,0").split(",").map(Number);
   432	    const cfg: ArchiveConfig = { ...CONFIG, seeds: CONFIG.screen.seeds, chain: { percents } };
   433	    const fx: ArchiveFixture = { kind: "historical-opponents-archive-fixture", version: 1, transferConditions: [], screenSeeds: cfg.screen.seeds, sources: [] };
   434	    const { runs, hex } = await generate(cfg, fx, nWorkers);
   435	    const panel = loadFirstRung();
   436	    const p = buildPayload(cfg, fx, runs, panel, hex, "", "", checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION);
   437	    const t = p.summary.chain.tournament;
   438	    console.log(JSON.stringify({ percents, label: p.summary.chain.fixture.label, rDir: t.analysis.rank.rDir, rDirResid: t.analysis.residRank?.rDir, payoff: t.payoff, edges: t.edges, gw: p.summary.gw }, null, 1));
   439	    console.log(`cpu ${cpu()} s`);
   440	  } else if (mode === "--mini" && process.argv.includes("--print")) {
   441	    const { cfg, fx } = mini();
   442	    const { runs, hex } = await generate(cfg, fx, nWorkers);
   443	    const panel = loadFirstRung();
   444	    process.stdout.write(serialize(sealPayload(buildPayload(cfg, fx, runs, panel, hex, "", "", checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION))));
   445	  } else {
   446	    console.error("usage: node tools/opponents/archive.ts --select | --freeze-check | --write | --check | --replay-check | --calibrate-chain | --mini --print");
   447	    process.exit(2);
   448	  }
   449	}

     1	// Archive tournament, pure part: snapshot selection, the descriptive directional rank against relabelled times, a general
     2	// directed-cycle detector, the temporal verdict table and the pure-matrix fixtures. No simulator import. Not in the metrics barrel.
     3	// Reuses the first rung's payoff statistics (payoff.ts) and does not copy them. Everything here is DESCRIPTIVE: the archive is
     4	// a set of related surviving elites, so a rank against relabelled times is a rank, never a p-value.
     5	import { findCycles, summarizePair, type BlockPayoff, type PairSummary } from "../opponents/payoff.ts";
     6	
     7	export const RANK_EPS = 1e-12;
     8	
     9	// ---- snapshot selection --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	
    11	export interface Elite { cell: [number, number]; born: number; quality: number; hex: string }
    12	export interface Bin { bin: number; lo: number; hi: number; status: "viable" | "empty"; pick: Elite | null; candidates: number; screened: number }
    13	
    14	/** Bin of a `born` batch index: K equal-width bins over [0, H). */
    15	export const binOf = (born: number, horizon: number, bins: number): number => Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((born * bins) / horizon));
    16	/** Horizon in batches: max(born) + 1. `born` is the bootstrap search's batch index, not an evaluation count. */
    17	export const horizonOf = (elites: readonly Pick<Elite, "born">[]): number => elites.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.born), -1) + 1;
    18	
    19	/** Highest quality first, ties by cell then born (all frozen). */
    20	export const byQuality = (a: Elite, b: Elite): number =>
    21	  b.quality - a.quality || a.cell[0] - b.cell[0] || a.cell[1] - b.cell[1] || a.born - b.born;
    22	
    23	/** Pure function of the elites and the viability predicate; the predicate is called lazily, best candidate first, once per elite. */
    24	export function selectSnapshots(elites: readonly Elite[], bins: number, isViable: (e: Elite) => boolean): { horizon: number; bins: Bin[] } {
    25	  const horizon = horizonOf(elites);
    26	  const out: Bin[] = [];
    27	  for (let k = 0; k < bins; k++) {
    28	    const cands = elites.filter((e) => binOf(e.born, horizon, bins) === k).sort(byQuality);
    29	    let pick: Elite | null = null, screened = 0;
    30	    for (const e of cands) { screened++; if (isViable(e)) { pick = e; break; } }
    31	    out.push({ bin: k, lo: Math.ceil((k * horizon) / bins), hi: Math.ceil(((k + 1) * horizon) / bins), status: pick ? "viable" : "empty", pick, candidates: cands.length, screened });
    32	  }
    33	  return { horizon, bins: out };
    34	}
    35	export const viableBins = (bins: readonly Bin[]): number => bins.filter((b) => b.status === "viable").length;
    36	
    37	// ---- directed cycles -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38	
    39	/** First directed cycle of any length (edges[i][j]: i beats j), as node indices, or null. Agrees with findCycles on triangles. */
    40	export function directedCycle(edges: readonly (readonly boolean[])[]): number[] | null {
    41	  const n = edges.length, state: number[] = new Array(n).fill(0), stack: number[] = [];
    42	  let found: number[] | null = null;
    43	  const visit = (u: number): void => {
    44	    state[u] = 1; stack.push(u);
    45	    for (let v = 0; v < n && !found; v++) {
    46	      if (!edges[u][v]) continue;
    47	      if (state[v] === 1) { found = stack.slice(stack.indexOf(v)); break; }
    48	      if (state[v] === 0) visit(v);
    49	    }
    50	    stack.pop(); state[u] = 2;
    51	  };
    52	  for (let u = 0; u < n && !found; u++) if (state[u] === 0) visit(u);
    53	  return found;
    54	}
    55	export { findCycles };
    56	
    57	// ---- directional statistic ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    58	
    59	/** Every permutation of 0..n-1 (lexicographic). perm[s] = the time rank given to snapshot s. */
    60	export function permutations(n: number): number[][] {
    61	  const out: number[][] = [], cur: number[] = [], used = new Array(n).fill(false);
    62	  const go = (): void => {
    63	    if (cur.length === n) { out.push([...cur]); return; }
    64	    for (let v = 0; v < n; v++) if (!used[v]) { used[v] = true; cur.push(v); go(); cur.pop(); used[v] = false; }
    65	  };
    66	  go();
    67	  return out;
    68	}
    69	
    70	export type Mat = (number | null)[][];
    71	/**
    72	 * D(perm) = mean over informative pairs of (payoff of the later-labelled snapshot - 0.5). `m[i][j]` is i's payoff against j
    73	 * (upper triangle read; null = uninformative, excluded and never read as 0.5). The informative mask belongs to the pair, so
    74	 * it travels with the snapshots under relabelling and the number of terms is the same for every permutation.
    75	 */
    76	export function directional(m: Mat, perm: readonly number[], centre = 0.5): { d: number | null; terms: number } {
    77	  let sum = 0, terms = 0;
    78	  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) {
    79	    const v = m[i][j];
    80	    if (v === null) continue;
    81	    sum += perm[j] > perm[i] ? centre - v : v - centre; // value of the later one, minus the centre (0.5 for payoffs, 0 for residuals)
    82	    terms++;
    83	  }
    84	  return { d: terms ? sum / terms : null, terms };
    85	}
    86	export interface Rank { observed: number | null; terms: number; perms: number; rDir: number | null; rRev: number | null; minRank: number }
    87	/** r_dir = #{D_perm >= D_obs} / n! (identity counted); r_rev = #{D_perm <= D_obs} / n!. Ranks against relabelled times, not p-values. */
    88	export function permutationRank(m: Mat, centre = 0.5): Rank {
    89	  const n = m.length, perms = permutations(n);
    90	  const obs = directional(m, perms[0], centre); // lexicographic first is the identity
    91	  if (obs.d === null) return { observed: null, terms: 0, perms: perms.length, rDir: null, rRev: null, minRank: 1 / perms.length };
    92	  let ge = 0, le = 0;
    93	  for (const p of perms) {
    94	    const d = directional(m, p, centre).d as number;
    95	    if (d >= obs.d - RANK_EPS) ge++;
    96	    if (d <= obs.d + RANK_EPS) le++;
    97	  }
    98	  return { observed: obs.d, terms: obs.terms, perms: perms.length, rDir: ge / perms.length, rRev: le / perms.length, minRank: 1 / perms.length };
    99	}
   100	/** Histogram input for the page: every permuted D (sorted ascending), rounded to 1e-9 so the file is byte stable. */
   101	export function permutedValues(m: Mat, centre = 0.5): number[] {
   102	  return permutations(m.length).map((p) => directional(m, p, centre).d).filter((d): d is number => d !== null).map((d) => Math.round(d * 1e9) / 1e9).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   103	}
   104	
   105	/** The naive reading: fraction of scheduled (later, earlier) pairs where the later snapshot's payoff exceeds 0.5. Uninformative pairs are not wins. */
   106	export function naiveLaterWins(m: Mat): { wins: number; pairs: number; fraction: number } {
   107	  let wins = 0, pairs = 0;
   108	  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) { pairs++; const v = m[i][j]; if (v !== null && 1 - v > 0.5 + RANK_EPS) wins++; }
   109	  return { wins, pairs, fraction: pairs ? wins / pairs : 0 };
   110	}

   113	
   114	export interface MatrixInput {
   115	  /** Payoff of row snapshot against column snapshot, upper triangle (i < j; i is the earlier). Null: uninformative. */
   116	  payoff: Mat;
   117	  /** Vigour residual (observed minus solo-predicted share) in the same layout, or null when solo runs are unavailable. */
   118	  resid: Mat | null;
   119	  /** edges[i][j]: i beats j by a confirmed `PairSummary.edge`. */
   120	  edges: boolean[][];
   121	}
   122	export interface Analysis {
   123	  n: number; scheduled: number; informative: number;
   124	  naive: { wins: number; pairs: number; fraction: number };
   125	  rank: Rank; residRank: Rank | null;
   126	  edgeCount: number; cycle: number[] | null; cycleFlag: "cycle" | "acyclic"; trianglesFound: number; ordered: boolean;
   127	}
   128	export const ORDER_MIN_EDGES = 3;
   129	export function analyze(inp: MatrixInput): Analysis {
   130	  const n = inp.payoff.length;
   131	  const rank = permutationRank(inp.payoff);
   132	  let residRank: Rank | null = null;
   133	  if (inp.resid) {
   134	    // the residual is only defined where the payoff is
   135	    const r: Mat = inp.resid.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => (inp.payoff[i][j] === null ? null : v)));
   136	    residRank = permutationRank(r, 0);
   137	  }
   138	  const cycle = directedCycle(inp.edges);
   139	  const edgeCount = inp.edges.reduce((s, row) => s + row.filter(Boolean).length, 0);
   140	  let informative = 0;
   141	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) if (inp.payoff[i][j] !== null) informative++;
   142	  return {
   143	    n, scheduled: (n * (n - 1)) / 2, informative, naive: naiveLaterWins(inp.payoff), rank, residRank, edgeCount, cycle,
   144	    cycleFlag: cycle ? "cycle" : "acyclic", trianglesFound: findCycles(inp.edges).length, ordered: !cycle && edgeCount >= ORDER_MIN_EDGES,
   145	  };
   146	}
   147	
   148	export type TemporalLabel =
   149	  | "control-invalid" | "insensitive-or-biased" | "assay-failed" | "partial-tournament"
   150	  | "directional-rank" | "directional-vigour-only" | "ordered-not-temporal" | "nothing-beyond-null";
   151	export const TEMPORAL_LABELS: readonly TemporalLabel[] = [
   152	  "control-invalid", "insensitive-or-biased", "assay-failed", "partial-tournament", "directional-rank", "directional-vigour-only", "ordered-not-temporal", "nothing-beyond-null",
   153	];
   154	export interface LabelInput {
   155	  controlInvalid: boolean; insensitiveOrBiased: boolean; assayFailed: boolean;
   156	  scheduled: number; informative: number; rDir: number | null; rDirResid: number | null; ordered: boolean; rDirMax: number;
   157	}
   158	/** Rows 5 to 7 of the verdict table. */
   159	export function bodyLabel(i: Pick<LabelInput, "rDir" | "rDirResid" | "ordered" | "rDirMax">): "directional-rank" | "directional-vigour-only" | "ordered-not-temporal" | "nothing-beyond-null" {
   160	  if (i.rDir !== null && i.rDir <= i.rDirMax) return i.rDirResid !== null && i.rDirResid <= i.rDirMax ? "directional-rank" : "directional-vigour-only";
   161	  return i.ordered ? "ordered-not-temporal" : "nothing-beyond-null";
   162	}
   163	/** Frozen precedence: rows 1 to 4 first match wins; `underlying` is the body label of a partial tournament. */
   164	export function temporalLabel(i: LabelInput): { label: TemporalLabel; underlying: TemporalLabel | null } {
   165	  if (i.controlInvalid) return { label: "control-invalid", underlying: null };
   166	  if (i.insensitiveOrBiased) return { label: "insensitive-or-biased", underlying: null };
   167	  if (i.assayFailed) return { label: "assay-failed", underlying: null };
   168	  const body = bodyLabel(i);
   169	  if (i.informative < i.scheduled) return { label: "partial-tournament", underlying: body };
   170	  return { label: body, underlying: null };
   171	}
   172	
   173	// ---- synthetic and planted fixtures ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   174	
   175	/** Ten blocks around `centre` (offsets -.01, 0, +.01 repeating, summing to zero), so the first rung's edge rule can fire on them. */
   176	export const tenBlocks = (centre: number): BlockPayoff[] =>
   177	  Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => { const v = centre + [-0.01, 0, 0.01, -0.01, 0, 0.01, -0.01, 0, 0.01, 0][i]; return { seed: i, payoff: v, naive: v, shares: [v] }; });
   178	
   179	/** Matrix input from "i|j" -> payoff of i against j (i < j); pairs not listed are 0.5 with no edge. */
   180	export function matrixFromCentres(n: number, centres: Record<string, number>): MatrixInput {
   181	  const payoff: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
   182	  const edges = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(false));
   183	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
   184	    const s: PairSummary = summarizePair(tenBlocks(centres[`${i}|${j}`] ?? 0.5));
   185	    payoff[i][j] = s.mean;
   186	    if (s.edge === "A>B") edges[i][j] = true;
   187	    if (s.edge === "B>A") edges[j][i] = true;
   188	  }
   189	  return { payoff, resid: null, edges };
   190	}
   191	
   192	/** Reorder a full snapshot-by-snapshot matrix input so `order[t]` is the original index given time t (upper triangle rebuilt). */
   193	export function reorder(full: { payoff: Mat; resid: Mat | null; edges: boolean[][] }, order: readonly number[]): MatrixInput {
   194	  const n = order.length;
   195	  const mk = (src: Mat | null): Mat | null => {
   196	    if (!src) return null;
   197	    const o: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
   198	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
   199	      const a = order[i], b = order[j];
   200	      o[i][j] = src[a][b];
   201	    }
   202	    return o;
   203	  };
   204	  return { payoff: mk(full.payoff) as Mat, resid: mk(full.resid), edges: order.map((a) => order.map((b) => full.edges[a][b])) };
   205	}
   206	
   207	/** A panel matrix in full layout: payoff[i][j] defined for both i != j (mirror 1 - p), resid antisymmetric. */
   208	export interface PanelMatrix { ids: string[]; payoff: Mat; resid: Mat | null; edges: boolean[][]; meanPayoff: Record<string, number | null> }
   209	
   210	export interface FixtureResult {
   211	  id: string; synthetic: boolean; times: string[];
   212	  naive: { wins: number; pairs: number; fraction: number };
   213	  rDir: number | null; rDirResid: number | null; minRank: number; cycleFlag: "cycle" | "acyclic"; trianglesFound: number; ordered: boolean;
   214	  label: TemporalLabel; expected: string; pass: boolean; note: string;
   215	}
   216	const bodyOf = (a: Analysis, rDirMax: number): TemporalLabel => bodyLabel({ rDir: a.rank.rDir, rDirResid: a.residRank?.rDir ?? null, ordered: a.ordered, rDirMax });
   217	function result(id: string, synthetic: boolean, times: string[], a: Analysis, rDirMax: number, expected: string, pass: boolean, note: string): FixtureResult {
   218	  return {
   219	    id, synthetic, times, naive: a.naive, rDir: a.rank.rDir, rDirResid: a.residRank?.rDir ?? null, minRank: a.rank.minRank, cycleFlag: a.cycleFlag, trianglesFound: a.trianglesFound,
   220	    ordered: a.ordered, label: bodyOf(a, rDirMax), expected, pass, note,
   221	  };
   222	}
   223	
   224	/** Full-layout matrix of a panel under an order of its ids (time 0 first); used by the strength-not-time fixture and its freeze-time search. */
   225	export function panelUnderOrder(panel: PanelMatrix, order: readonly string[]): MatrixInput {
   226	  const idx = order.map((id) => panel.ids.indexOf(id));
   227	  return reorder(panel, idx);
   228	}
   229	/** Order of ids by ascending mean payoff (weakest first): the strength-aligned time assignment. Ties by id. */
   230	export const strengthOrder = (panel: PanelMatrix): string[] =>
   231	  [...panel.ids].sort((a, b) => (panel.meanPayoff[a] ?? 0) - (panel.meanPayoff[b] ?? 0) || (a < b ? -1 : 1));
   232	
   233	export interface FixtureSpec { rDirMax: number; scrambled: string[]; panel: PanelMatrix }
   234	export function runFixtures(spec: FixtureSpec): FixtureResult[] {
   235	  const T = spec.rDirMax;
   236	  const out: FixtureResult[] = [];
   237	  // 1. a 3-cycle that the naive metric reads as improvement (2 of 3 pairs "later wins"); K = 3 floor is 1/6, so this validates the flag, not significance
   238	  const c3 = analyze(matrixFromCentres(3, { "0|1": 0.2, "1|2": 0.2, "0|2": 0.8 }));
   239	  out.push(result("cycle-fools-naive", true, ["A", "B", "C"], c3, T, "cycle flag raised; naive 2 of 3", c3.cycleFlag === "cycle" && c3.naive.wins === 2 && c3.naive.pairs === 3 && (c3.rank.rDir ?? 0) > T, "synthetic A<B<C<A; K=3 permutation floor is 1/6, so only the flag is validated"));
   240	  // 2. a 4-cycle plus a dominant newest member: no triangle, so the first rung's findCycles misses it
   241	  const long = analyze(matrixFromCentres(5, { "0|1": 0.1, "1|2": 0.1, "2|3": 0.1, "0|3": 0.56, "0|4": 0.2, "1|4": 0.2, "2|4": 0.2, "3|4": 0.2 }));
   242	  // time order D,C,B,A,E (indices 0..4): C beats D, B beats C, A beats B, D beats A, E beats all
   243	  out.push(result("long-cycle-fools-naive", true, ["D", "C", "B", "A", "E"], long, T, "cycle flag raised by the general detector; findCycles finds no triangle", long.cycleFlag === "cycle" && long.trianglesFound === 0 && long.naive.fraction >= 0.7, "synthetic; edges C>D, B>C, A>B at .9, D>A at .56, E beats every earlier snapshot at .8"));
   244	  // 3. vigour only: payoff V_i/(V_i+V_j), strengths 1..5 aligned with time; residual zero everywhere
   245	  const V = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5];
   246	  const vp: Mat = V.map((a, i) => V.map((b, j) => (j > i ? Math.round((1e9 * a) / (a + b)) / 1e9 : null)));
   247	  const vr: Mat = V.map((_, i) => V.map((_, j) => (j > i ? 0 : null)));
   248	  const vig = analyze({ payoff: vp, resid: vr, edges: V.map(() => V.map(() => false)) });
   249	  out.push(result("vigour-only", true, ["V1", "V2", "V3", "V4", "V5"], vig, T, "directional-vigour-only", bodyOf(vig, T) === "directional-vigour-only" && vig.rank.rDir === 1 / 120, "synthetic; every payoff is exactly the solo-growth share, so the residual has nothing to align"));
   250	  // 4. strength-not-time on the first rung's real panel matrix: aligned versus scrambled time assignment
   251	  const aligned = strengthOrder(spec.panel);
   252	  const al = analyze(panelUnderOrder(spec.panel, aligned));
   253	  const sc = analyze(panelUnderOrder(spec.panel, spec.scrambled));
   254	  const alLabel = bodyOf(al, T), scLabel = bodyOf(sc, T);
   255	  out.push(result("strength-aligned", false, aligned, al, T, "directional-rank or directional-vigour-only", alLabel === "directional-rank" || alLabel === "directional-vigour-only", "first rung's committed pairwise matrix, times in strength order (pure matrix, no new simulation)"));
   256	  out.push(result("strength-scrambled", false, spec.scrambled, sc, T, "ordered-not-temporal", scLabel === "ordered-not-temporal" && (sc.rank.rDir ?? 0) > 0.2, "same matrix, frozen scrambled time assignment; the naive metric still reads it as partly improving"));
   257	  return out;
   258	}
   259	
   260	/**
   261	 * The planted chain is in-world: classify it from its measured matrix. Must be directional (rank or vigour-only): the planted
   262	 * manipulation is a weakened photosynthesis weight, a vigour manipulation, so its residual need not align (calibration on the
   263	 * screen seeds showed r_dir = 1/24 with residual ranks 0.29 to 0.67). Every scheduled chain pair must be informative. The vigour split is gated by the vigour-only fixture.
   264	 */
   265	export function chainFixture(a: Analysis, rDirMax: number, times: string[]): FixtureResult {
   266	  return result("planted-chain", false, times, a, rDirMax, "directional-rank or directional-vigour-only", a.informative === a.scheduled && ["directional-rank", "directional-vigour-only"].includes(bodyOf(a, rDirMax)), "calibrated instrument genomes with graded weakening, times in order of strength (in-world positive control)");
   267	}
   268	
   269	/** Search used at freeze time: the first order (lexicographic over indices into the strength order) with rank above 0.5 that has the highest naive fraction. */
   270	export function chooseScrambled(panel: PanelMatrix, rDirMax: number): { order: string[]; rDir: number; naive: number } | null {
   271	  const base = strengthOrder(panel);
   272	  let best: { order: string[]; rDir: number; naive: number } | null = null;
   273	  for (const p of permutations(base.length)) {
   274	    const order = p.map((k) => base[k]);
   275	    const a = analyze(panelUnderOrder(panel, order));
   276	    const rd = a.rank.rDir ?? 0;
   277	    if (rd <= 0.2 || a.cycle || !a.ordered || rd <= rDirMax) continue;
   278	    if (!best || a.naive.fraction > best.naive) best = { order, rDir: rd, naive: a.naive.fraction };
   279	  }
   280	  return best;
   281	}
