  1930	# CLAUDE.md
  1931	
  1932	This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.
  1933	
  1934	## What this is
  1935	
  1936	An open-ended artificial-life ecology on WebGPU: closed in matter, open in energy, exact integer physics. Individuals, reproduction and heredity are *inferred* from local rules, never declared (no `Organism` class, no `reproduce()`). Research goal and milestones (M3 bootstrap → M4/M5 ensembles): `docs/plan.md`. Rules in prose plus the arithmetic-bounds argument: `docs/rules.md`.
  1937	
  1938	The repo is a pnpm workspace (TypeScript packages + Vite lab), a Deno toolchain for headless GPU runs, and a Phoenix app (`apps/coordinator`). It is jj-colocated with git (`.jj/`).
  1939	
  1940	## Commands
  1941	
  1942	```bash
  1943	pnpm install
  1944	pnpm dev                 # lab at http://localhost:5173 (/selftest.html, /island.html)
  1945	bin/dev                  # lab + coordinator together (PORT, LAB_PORT override 4000/5173)
  1946	pnpm typecheck           # tsc over packages/*/src, apps/lab/src, tests (excludes tests/deno)
  1947	pnpm test                # vitest: apps/lab/test, packages/*/test, experiments/test, tools/test
  1948	pnpm vitest run packages/sim-ref/test/ref.test.ts -t "<name>"   # single test
  1949	deno run -A tests/deno/gpu_golden.ts    # GPU vs CPU reference, bit for bit (native WebGPU)
  1950	npx playwright test      # browser golden (tests/e2e), Chrome with WebGPU flags; BL_ALL_ENGINES=1 adds firefox/webkit
  1951	BL_E2E_PORT=5241 npx playwright test   # a second worktree on its own port (default 5174; strictPort, never reuses a server)
  1952	cd apps/coordinator && mix test         # or `mix test test/coordinator/queue_test.exs`
  1953	cd apps/coordinator && mix precommit    # warnings-as-errors, format, test
  1954	pnpm gen:prereg          # regenerate experiments/preregistration.md generated sections
  1955	pnpm gen:cards           # redraw the link-preview cards (site + one per world) with Chrome/WebGPU; then look at them
  1956	```
  1957	
  1958	Run `pnpm install` before any `deno run` in a fresh checkout (deno 2.9 otherwise writes `workspaces` into package.json). Tests that start deno pass `--no-lock` (`tools/test/deno-no-lock.test.ts` checks; a launcher or coordinator a test starts that runs deno itself gets the PATH shim in `tests/deno/lib/no_lock_deno.ts`), but a top-level `deno run`, or a tool that starts deno, still writes a stale deno.lock: when package.json deps change, update it deliberately (`deno cache --frozen tools/stitch.ts` fails while it is stale).
  1959	
  1960	`tests/deno/*.ts` are standalone scripts (not `Deno.test`); run each with `deno run -A tests/deno/<name>.ts`. Several need a real GPU; `coordinator_integration.ts` spawns `mix phx.server` against a scratch data dir.
  1961	
  1962	Experiment CLIs (`tools/`, all Deno, native WebGPU): `run.ts` (write run bundles to `runs/<experiment>/<preset>/<condition>/seed-<n>/`), `analyze.ts` (ensemble analysis), `bootstrap.ts` (M3 MAP-Elites search), `retest.ts`, `calibrate.ts`, `island.ts` (headless island), `stitch.ts` (coordinator segments → bundles). Usage is in each file's header comment and in README.md. `runs/` is gitignored and can hold very large outputs.
  1963	
  1964	## Architecture
  1965	
  1966	**Package imports.** `@bl/*` resolve to `packages/*/src/index.ts` via four separate maps that must stay in sync: `tsconfig.json` paths, `vitest.config.ts` aliases, `deno.json` imports, and `apps/lab/vite.config.ts` aliases. There is no build step for packages.
  1967	
  1968	**Two implementations of one rule set.**
  1969	- `packages/sim-ref/src/step.ts` is the executable specification (CPU).
  1970	- `packages/sim-gpu/src/shaders.ts` (WGSL) must match it bit for bit. `packages/sim-gpu/src/golden.ts` defines golden cases and runs on any WebGPU host (Deno, browser `/selftest.html`, Playwright).
  1971	- `packages/sim-ref/test/golden-hashes.test.ts` pins state hashes per `RULE_VERSION` (in `packages/schema/src/config.ts`). Any change to dynamics changes the hashes: an intended rule change bumps `RULE_VERSION` and re-pins deliberately. A change that isn't meant to alter dynamics must leave every pin untouched — new optional `WorldConfig` keys stay absent from `defaultConfig()` so existing configs hash identically.
  1972	- All arithmetic is integer (u32 matter, fixed-point controllers, counter-based PRNG keyed on seed/step/cell, stochastic rounding with remainders to the source). Conservation residual is exactly zero. Keep new arithmetic inside the bounds listed in `docs/rules.md` (`MATTER_MAX`, `POOL_MAX`, `mulShr` limits); `validateConfig`/`validateState` enforce them.
  1973	
  1974	**Packages.** `schema` (config, presets, layout, genome, worlds, checkpoint encoding, migration/exchange, M3 founder set) → `sim-ref`, `sim-gpu` → `metrics` (census, tracker, collectives, activity, complexity, stats; runs on readbacks) → `runner` (conditions, `runExperiment`, observers, stitching, island client) and `search` (MAP-Elites, gates). `lineage` (schema, sim-ref) is the lineage inspector's genotype core: keys, ancestry, mutation replay, the controller probe, and the mutation-edge store the lab keeps. `runner` code runs unchanged in browsers and Deno.
  1975	
  1976	**Checkpoints and replay.** A checkpoint artifact carries physics state and observer state together, under one digest. Segmented runs must equal a continuous run byte for byte (`tests/deno/segments.ts`, `tests/deno/stitch.ts`); this determinism is what makes distributed verification possible.
  1977	
  1978	**Archipelago.** `apps/coordinator` (Phoenix, JSON API under `/api`, static status page `priv/static/index.html`) hands islands segment tasks. `Coordinator.Queue` does scheduling, `Coordinator.Segment` owns segment state transitions, and `Coordinator.Store` is content-addressed storage for checkpoints (`.blck`) and bundle files. A fraction of segments is re-run by a second island and compared by state hash. Islands are `apps/lab/island.html` (browser) or `tools/island.ts` (Deno), both via `packages/runner/src/island.ts`. `apps/coordinator/AGENTS.md` has Phoenix/Elixir/OTP conventions to follow there; everything between its `usage-rules` markers is generated by `mix usage_rules.sync` (config in `mix.exs`), so rerun that after dependency changes rather than editing the section. `mix usage_rules.search_docs "<term>" -p <pkg>` searches hexdocs for the coordinator's deps.
  1979	
  1980	**Lab.** `apps/lab/src/sim.worker.ts` owns the WebGPU device, frame loop, rendering and OPFS storage. `apps/lab/src/execution.ts` owns census cadence, observer integrity, checkpoint settlement and replay traversal. State-changing messages run through `exclusive()`, which waits for in-flight frame execution; generation identity invalidates stale readbacks.
  1981	
  1982	**Pre-registration.** `experiments/endpoints.ts` is the source of truth for primary endpoints, held-out observables and activity thresholds, and `tools/analyze.ts` executes it. The generated sections of `experiments/preregistration.md` (between `GENERATED` markers) come from `pnpm gen:prereg`, and `experiments/test/prereg-sync.test.ts` fails when they are stale. Edit `endpoints.ts` and regenerate; never hand-edit inside the markers. The doc is frozen (2026-09-27): its SHA-256 is recorded in `experiments/FROZEN`, `pnpm gen:prereg` now refuses to rewrite the generated sections, and every change goes in a dated amendment section.
  1983	
  1984	**Analysis scale.** Run bundles can be hundreds of MB per run (for example `lineages.tsv`). Analysis tools stream per census rather than loading whole tables; keep new analysis code streaming.
  1985	
  1986	## Codex reviews
  1987	
  1988	Reviews by Codex use **Sol 6.1 at High effort**, not Astra (standing instruction from 2026-10-03, replaces the earlier `gpt-6-astra` policy). Run them read-only at phase boundaries and always before committing:
  1989	
  1990	```bash
  1991	codex exec -m gpt-6.1-sol -c model_reasoning_effort=high -s read-only -C <checkout> - < prompt.md > review.txt
  1992	```
  1993	
  1994	Use `< /dev/null` when the prompt is an argument (otherwise codex waits on stdin) and `-o <file>` for the final message. Historical "Astra review" comments in the code are provenance for past reviews; leave them. Delegated agents and workspaces follow the same rule.
  1995	- `no pooled rank or p across sources`; `shared genomes across sources are flagged`; `born binning uses batch index (regression on the real archive ranges)`.
  1996	- `first rung --check still passes with the new library present` (assay.ts source enumeration unaffected).
  1997	- `seeds are disjoint from search 1..300, screen, first-rung and calibration seeds`.
  1998	- `schedule counts match config` (200 contests per source, solo, controls).
  1999	- `planted-chain in-world mini (T=60, 2 seeds) orders by weakening` and `twin snapshot swap is exact` (small real arena runs, reusing `runJob`).
  2000	
  2001	`tools/test/opponents-archive-evidence.test.ts`
  2002	- `evidence seal and manifest verify` (`--check` returns no problems; no timestamps/platform strings; statements include "CPU only", "no GPU evidence", "not the M3 environment", "archive time is search time").
  2003	- `tampered payload, manifest, archive fixture or source is rejected`; `resealed but inconsistent payload is rejected`.
  2004	- `verdict in evidence equals the verdict recomputed from stored gates`.
  2005	- `freeze-check passes on the real branch and fails when an outcome file is in the freeze commit or an inventoried code file changes` (temp git repo fixture).
  2006	- `worker count does not change output` (mini config at `--jobs 1` and `3`, byte equal); `mini regeneration equals itself`.
  2007	- `archive fixture hexes decode and match source file SHA-256 when RUNS is present` (skipped with a stated reason when `runs/` is absent, since CI has no runs/).
  2008	
  2009	`apps/lab/test/opponents-archive-packaging.test.ts`: Vite input present, Caddy private route matches `/opponents/archive/` and `/OPPONENTS/archive/index.html`, Dockerfile removal covers the dist path, page text carries "CPU only", "no GPU evidence", "noindex", a captioned table. `apps/lab/test/opponents-archive-render.test.ts`: matrix numbers equal the table; `uninformative` is its own state; naive and sealed metrics both shown per fixture; permutation histogram observed marker matches evidence; text says synthetic for the cycle fixture. `tests/e2e/opponents-archive.spec.ts` (Playwright, `BL_E2E_PORT=5331`): page loads from the committed evidence, no console errors, no horizontal overflow at 375 px, screenshots at 1280 and 375 saved to `docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/` and read.

  2028	
  2029	## 7. How to try it
  2030	
  2031	```bash
  2032	export PATH=~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.2.0/bin:$PATH
  2033	cd /Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life-i03-archive-tournament
  2034	pnpm install
  2035	pnpm dev -- --port 5321 --strictPort
  2036	# open http://localhost:5321/opponents/archive/   (linked from /opponents/); private, noindex, local only
  2037	node tools/opponents/archive.ts --check              # verify saved evidence, no simulation
  2038	RUNS=/Users/nicholas/develop/browser-life/runs node tools/opponents/archive.ts --replay-check --jobs 8   # regenerate and compare, minutes
  2039	```
  2040	On the page: pick a source to see its later-versus-earlier matrix; read the directional statistic against its permutation null; open the control strip (identical swap, weakened copy, starved arena); open "where a naive metric is fooled" to see the cycle and strength-not-time fixtures with both numbers side by side.
  2041	
  2042	## 8. Non-goals and limits
  2043	
  2044	- No evolving against an archive, no recent-only/fixed-panel/mixture comparison, no withheld-opponent generality, no whole-patch or ecological-context transfer, no predation or reproduction claim, no open-endedness claim.
  2045	- Archive time is search evaluation index; archives are surviving elites, not a full history; sources are three search runs, so intervals are wide and "nothing" means none detected at this resolution.
  2046	- Biomass share at step 300 in a small CPU arena with a single chemistry; balanced arm only; no GPU or physical-hardware evidence.
  2047	- No physics, schema, preset, `RULE_VERSION` or `defaultConfig()` change; no new `WorldConfig` keys; no paid calls, AWS, deploy; the plan stage commits nothing.
  2048	- If scope must shrink, cut in this order: third source, `strength-not-time` in-world version (keep the pure matrix), the Playwright spec (keep render tests), residual-based statistic.
  2049	
  2050	## 9. Order of work
  2051	
  2052	1. Library with pure functions and tests (selection, permutation null, verdict, fixtures). 2. Snapshot selection and viability screen from `RUNS` into `archive.json` and `archive-config.json`. 3. CLI (`--freeze-check`, mini config). 4. Codex review of freeze files, fix, commit only freeze files, `git push -u origin claude/i03-archive-tournament`. 5. `--write` the evidence, `--replay-check`. 6. Page, packaging edits, render and e2e tests, screenshots. 7. `EVIDENCE.md` (result, deviations, cost), full gates, Codex source review.
  2053	
  2054	## v3 pre-freeze amendment (2026-11-02, IMPLEMENT stage, before any scoring seed was run)
  2055	
  2056	Made after chain calibration on screen seeds 901..903 only (`chain-calibration.json`, 7 trials) and before the freeze commit. No scoring seed (1001..1010) had been run.
  2057	
  2058	1. **Planted-chain gate (section 2.2 and verdict row 3).** v2 required the planted chain to be `directional-rank`. Six of seven calibration trials reached `r_dir` at the K=4 floor (1/24; the seventh, `[70,45,20,0]`, was 2/24 and `nothing-beyond-null`) and none had an aligned vigour residual (residual ranks 0.29 to 0.67), because the planted manipulation (a weakened photosynthesis weight) is a vigour manipulation by construction. The gate is now: planted chain is `directional-rank` or `directional-vigour-only` (it must be directional; the vigour split is gated by the `vigour-only` fixture). Selected chain: weakening percents `[80,60,40,0]`, time in ascending strength.
  2059	2. **Stated consequence.** No fixture or control reaches `directional-rank` (strength-aligned on the first rung's panel is also `directional-vigour-only`, residual rank 0.092). The top label has no positive control; a real `directional-rank` verdict would be unvalidated and is recorded as a limit in the evidence.
  2060	3. **strength-not-time in-world rerun is cut** (allowed by section 8's cut order); the pure-matrix fixture on the first rung's committed matrix is the gate. Scrambled order frozen in `archive-config.json`: `F3,F2,G,F1,W` (rank 0.25, naive later-wins fraction 0.7, ordered-not-temporal).
  2061	4. **Vigour residual directional statistic** uses centre 0 (later minus earlier residual), not 0.5; implemented in `permutationRank(m, centre)`.
  2062	5. **Selection outcome** (screen seeds 901..903, 13 genomes screened, all passed): all three sources kept with five viable bins each (`bootstrap-m3` horizon 80, `bootstrap-200` horizon 298, `bootstrap-div` horizon 80). The first-ranked elite of bin 2 of `bootstrap-m3` is byte-identical to bin 0 of `bootstrap-200` and bin 2 of `bootstrap-div` (flagged `shared-genome`; none of the three sources is wholly shared, so none is dropped). Elite `quality` is saturated near 1.0 for most elites, so ties (broken by cell then born) decide many bins.
  2063	6. **Ordering of "ordered" strength**: `ordered` means no directed cycle among confirmed edges and at least 3 confirmed edges (a topological strength order exists).
  2064	7. **Review-driven changes before freeze (Sol xhigh freeze review, 8 findings)**: the freeze inventory now includes `stats.ts`, all schema and sim-ref sources, PLAN.md and chain-calibration.json; `--check` verifies freeze provenance (ancestor, inventory, manifest commit); `--replay-check` runs the full saved-evidence and freeze verification before any simulation and refuses without evidence; the planted-chain gate requires every scheduled chain pair informative; viability flags in `archive.json` are recomputed from the recorded counts; test-only seeds 7001, 7002 replace the reserved scoring seeds in pre-freeze tests.
  2065

  2204	    80	export function planOf(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture): Plan {
  2205	    81	  const jobs: JobSpec[] = [];
  2206	    82	  const bal = (a: string, b: string, depletion = false): void => {
  2207	    83	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const pos of ["P1", "P2"] as const) {
  2208	    84	      const s = ARMS.bal[pos];
  2209	    85	      jobs.push({ key: `${depletion ? "d" : "c"}|${pk(a, b)}|${depletion ? "" : "bal|"}${pos}|${seed}`, seed, a, b, slotsA: [...s.A], slotsB: [...s.B], depletion });
  2210	    86	    }
  2211	    87	  };
  2212	    88	  const solo = (id: string): void => {
  2213	    89	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const m of ["m01", "m23"]) jobs.push({ key: `s|${id}|${m}|${seed}`, seed, a: id, b: null, slotsA: MASKS[m], slotsB: [], depletion: false });
  2214	    90	  };
  2215	    91	  const sources: Plan["sources"] = [];
  2216	    92	  for (const s of keptSources(fx)) {
  2217	    93	    const ids = s.snapshots.map((x) => x.id);
  2218	    94	    sources.push({ short: s.short, ids });
  2219	    95	    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < ids.length; j++) bal(ids[i], ids[j]);
  2220	    96	    ids.forEach(solo);
  2221	    97	    const L = ids[ids.length - 1];
  2222	    98	    bal(L, twinOf(L));
  2223	    99	    bal(L, weakId(L));
  2224	   100	    solo(weakId(L));
  2225	   101	  }
  2226	   102	  // calibrated weakened-copy gate and the planted chain (instrument genomes)
  2227	   103	  bal("G", "W");
  2228	   104	  solo("G"); solo("W");
  2229	   105	  const chain = cfg.chain.percents.map((_, k) => chainId(k));
  2230	   106	  for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < chain.length; j++) bal(chain[i], chain[j]);
  2231	   107	  chain.forEach(solo);
  2232	   108	  const first = sources[0];
  2233	   109	  const starved: [string, string] | null = first && first.ids.length >= 2 ? [first.ids[0], first.ids[1]] : null;
  2234	   110	  if (starved) bal(starved[0], starved[1], true);
  2235	   111	  jobs.sort((x, y) => (x.key < y.key ? -1 : x.key > y.key ? 1 : 0));
  2236	   112	  const seen = new Set<string>();
  2237	   113	  for (const j of jobs) { if (seen.has(j.key)) throw new Error(`duplicate job key ${j.key}`); seen.add(j.key); }
  2238	   114	  return { jobs, sources, starved, chain };
  2239	   115	}

  2249	   125	type Index = Map<string, RunRec>;
  2250	   126	function blocksFor(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number, kind: "c" | "d" = "c"): BlockPayoff[] {
  2251	   127	  return seeds.map((seed) => {
  2252	   128	    const mk = (pos: string): RunRec => {
  2253	   129	      const r = idx.get(kind === "c" ? `c|${pk(a, b)}|bal|${pos}|${seed}` : `d|${pk(a, b)}|${pos}|${seed}`);
  2254	   130	      if (!r) throw new Error(`missing run ${pk(a, b)} ${pos} ${seed}`);
  2255	   131	      return r;
  2256	   132	    };

  2259	   135	}
  2260	   136	/** Mean over seeds of observed payoff minus solo-predicted share; null when no seed is usable. */
  2261	   137	function residualOf(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number): { observed: number; expected: number; residual: number; blocks: number } | null {
  2262	   138	  const per: { payoff: number; expected: number }[] = [];
  2263	   139	  for (const seed of seeds) {
  2264	   140	    const blk = blocksFor(idx, a, b, [seed], k)[0];
  2265	   141	    if (blk.payoff === null) continue;
  2266	   142	    const v = (id: string, m: string): number => (idx.get(`s|${id}|${m}|${seed}`) as RunRec).cp[k][0];
  2267	   143	    const e1 = vigourExpected(v(a, "m01"), v(b, "m23")), e2 = vigourExpected(v(a, "m23"), v(b, "m01"));
  2268	   144	    if (e1 === null || e2 === null) continue;
  2269	   145	    per.push({ payoff: blk.payoff, expected: (e1 + e2) / 2 });
  2270	   146	  }
  2271	   147	  return per.length ? { observed: mean(per.map((x) => x.payoff)), expected: mean(per.map((x) => x.expected)), residual: mean(per.map((x) => x.payoff - x.expected)), blocks: per.length } : null;
  2272	   148	}

  2282	   158	  permuted: number[];
  2283	   159	  /** Descriptive extras: solo growth at the primary checkpoint per snapshot (mean over masks and seeds) and its step-zero biomass. */
  2284	   160	  solo: Dict<{ mean: number; b0: number; aliveAll: boolean }>;
  2285	   161	}
  2286	   162	/** One tournament over `ids` in time order. */
  2287	   163	export function tournament(idx: Index, ids: string[], seeds: number[], k: number): TournamentSummary {
  2288	   164	  const n = ids.length;
  2289	   165	  const payoff: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
  2290	   166	  const resid: Mat = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(null));
  2291	   167	  const edges = Array.from({ length: n }, () => new Array(n).fill(false));
  2292	   168	  const pairs: PairRec[] = [];
  2293	   169	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
  2294	   170	    const summary = summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, ids[i], ids[j], seeds, k), COMPLETE_FRACTION.balanced);
  2295	   171	    const vigour = residualOf(idx, ids[i], ids[j], seeds, k);
  2296	   172	    pairs.push({ a: ids[i], b: ids[j], summary, vigour });
  2297	   173	    if (summary.status === "informative" && summary.mean !== null) {
  2298	   174	      payoff[i][j] = summary.mean;
  2299	   175	      resid[i][j] = vigour ? vigour.residual : null;
  2300	   176	    }
  2301	   177	    if (summary.edge === "A>B") edges[i][j] = true;
  2302	   178	    if (summary.edge === "B>A") edges[j][i] = true;
  2303	   179	  }
  2304	   180	  const analysis = analyze({ payoff, resid, edges });
  2305	   181	  const solo: TournamentSummary["solo"] = {};
  2306	   182	  for (const id of ids) {
  2307	   183	    const rs = [...idx.values()].filter((r) => r.key.startsWith(`s|${id}|`));
  2308	   184	    solo[id] = { mean: mean(rs.map((r) => r.cp[k][0])), b0: mean(rs.map((r) => r.b0[0])), aliveAll: rs.every((r) => !atFloor(r.cp[k][0], r.b0[0])) };
  2309	   185	  }
  2310	   186	  return { ids, pairs, payoff, resid, edges, analysis, permuted: permutedValues(payoff), solo };
  2311	   187	}
  2312	   188	
  2313	   189	export interface TwinSummary { id: string; labelInvariance: boolean; positionAdvantage: (number | null)[]; signFlipP: number | null; meanAbs: number | null; effectTarget: number; positionOk: boolean }
  2314	   190	function twinSummary(idx: Index, id: string, seeds: number[], k: number, target: number): TwinSummary {
  2315	   191	  const t = twinOf(id);
  2316	   192	  let inv = true;
  2317	   193	  const adv: (number | null)[] = [];
  2318	   194	  for (const seed of seeds) {
  2319	   195	    const r1 = idx.get(`c|${pk(id, t)}|bal|P1|${seed}`) as RunRec, r2 = idx.get(`c|${pk(id, t)}|bal|P2|${seed}`) as RunRec;
  2320	   196	    // same genome under two labels: the reciprocal worlds are one physical world, so counts swap exactly
  2321	   197	    if (r1.b0[0] !== r2.b0[1] || r1.b0[1] !== r2.b0[0]) inv = false;
  2322	   198	    r1.cp.forEach((c, kk) => { const d = r2.cp[kk]; if (c[0] !== d[1] || c[1] !== d[0] || c[2] !== d[3] || c[3] !== d[2]) inv = false; });
  2323	   199	    const o = obs(r1, k);
  2324	   200	    adv.push(o.bA + o.bB === 0 ? null : o.bA / (o.bA + o.bB) - o.b0A / (o.b0A + o.b0B));
  2325	   201	  }
  2326	   202	  const ok = adv.filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
  2327	   203	  const p = ok.length === adv.length ? signFlipP(ok) : null;
  2328	   204	  const meanAbs = ok.length === adv.length && ok.length ? mean(ok.map(Math.abs)) : null;
  2329	   205	  return { id, labelInvariance: inv, positionAdvantage: adv, signFlipP: p, meanAbs, effectTarget: target, positionOk: p !== null && p > 0.05 && meanAbs !== null && meanAbs < target };
  2330	   206	}
  2331	   207	
  2332	   208	export interface WeakSummary { a: string; b: string; complete: number; allBelowHalf: boolean; meanPayoffB: number | null; soloAboveFloor: boolean | null; detected: boolean }
  2333	   209	function weakSummary(idx: Index, a: string, b: string, seeds: number[], k: number, soloCheck: boolean): WeakSummary {
  2334	   210	  const s = summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, a, b, seeds, k));
  2335	   211	  const wp = s.payoffs.map((x) => 1 - x);
  2336	   212	  const alive = soloCheck ? ["m01", "m23"].every((m) => seeds.every((sd) => { const r = idx.get(`s|${b}|${m}|${sd}`) as RunRec; return !atFloor(r.cp[k][0], r.b0[0]); })) : null;
  2337	   213	  const allBelowHalf = wp.length > 0 && wp.every((x) => x < 0.5);
  2338	   214	  const meanB = wp.length ? mean(wp) : null;
  2339	   215	  return { a, b, complete: wp.length, allBelowHalf, meanPayoffB: meanB, soloAboveFloor: alive, detected: allBelowHalf && wp.length >= EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS && meanB !== null && meanB <= G2_MEAN_MAX && alive !== false };
  2340	   216	}
  2341	   217	
  2342	   218	export interface SourceSummary {
  2343	   219	  short: string; name: string; tournament: TournamentSummary; twin: TwinSummary;
  2344	   220	  /** Descriptive: weakened copy of the latest snapshot, with its solo viability. */
  2345	   221	  weakCopy: WeakSummary;
  2346	   222	  label: TemporalLabel; underlying: TemporalLabel | null;
  2347	   223	  flags: { controlInvalid: boolean; insensitiveOrBiased: boolean };
  2348	   224	}
  2349	   225	export interface Starved { pair: [string, string]; bothBelowFloorEveryRun: boolean; status: string; inventories: { seed: number; pos: string; fracA: number; fracB: number }[] }
  2350	   226	export interface Summary {
  2351	   227	  checkpoint: number;
  2352	   228	  sources: SourceSummary[];
  2353	   229	  gw: WeakSummary;
  2354	   230	  starved: Starved | null;
  2355	   231	  chain: { ids: string[]; percents: number[]; tournament: TournamentSummary; fixture: FixtureResult };
  2356	   232	  fixtures: FixtureResult[];
  2357	   233	  naiveVersusSealed: { id: string; naive: number; label: TemporalLabel }[];
  2358	   234	  global: { runsValid: boolean; starvedOk: boolean; gwDetected: boolean; assayOk: boolean };
  2359	   235	  labelCounts: Dict<number>;
  2360	   236	}
  2361	   237	export const labelsOf = (s: Summary): Dict<string> => Object.fromEntries(s.sources.map((x) => [x.short, x.label]));
  2362	   238	
  2363	   239	export interface FirstRung { ids: string[]; payoff: Mat; edges: boolean[][]; resid: Mat | null; meanPayoff: Dict<number | null> }
  2364	   240	/** Full-layout panel matrix from the first rung's committed summary (payoff mirrored, residual antisymmetric). */
  2365	   241	export function panelFromFirstRung(p: { summary: { matrix: { ids: string[]; payoff: Mat; edges: boolean[][]; meanPayoff: Dict<number | null> }; vigour: Dict<{ residual: number | null } | null> } }): PanelMatrix {
  2366	   242	  const { ids, payoff, edges, meanPayoff } = p.summary.matrix;
  2367	   243	  const resid: Mat = ids.map(() => ids.map(() => null));
  2368	   244	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(p.summary.vigour)) {
  2369	   245	    const [a, b] = k.split("|");
  2370	   246	    const i = ids.indexOf(a), j = ids.indexOf(b);
  2371	   247	    if (v && v.residual !== null) { resid[i][j] = v.residual; resid[j][i] = -v.residual; }
  2372	   248	  }
  2373	   249	  return { ids, payoff, resid, edges, meanPayoff };
  2374	   250	}
  2375	   251	
  2376	   252	export function summarize(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, runs: RunRec[], panel: PanelMatrix, checkpoints: number[]): Summary {
  2377	   253	  const idx: Index = new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
  2378	   254	  const plan = planOf(cfg, fx);
  2379	   255	  if (plan.jobs.length !== runs.length || plan.jobs.some((j) => !idx.has(j.key))) throw new Error("run records do not match the schedule");
  2380	   256	  const k = checkpoints.length - 1;
  2381	   257	  const runsValid = validateRuns({ arena: cfg.arena } as never, runs, checkpoints).length === 0;
  2382	   258	  const gw = weakSummary(idx, "G", "W", cfg.seeds, k, true);
  2383	   259	  let starved: Starved | null = null;
  2384	   260	  if (plan.starved) {
  2385	   261	    const [a, b] = plan.starved;
  2386	   262	    const inventories: Starved["inventories"] = [];
  2387	   263	    let all = true;
  2388	   264	    for (const seed of cfg.seeds) for (const pos of ["P1", "P2"]) {
  2389	   265	      const o = obs(idx.get(`d|${pk(a, b)}|${pos}|${seed}`) as RunRec, k);
  2390	   266	      inventories.push({ seed, pos, fracA: o.bA / o.b0A, fracB: o.bB / o.b0B });
  2391	   267	      if (!(atFloor(o.bA, o.b0A) && atFloor(o.bB, o.b0B))) all = false;
  2392	   268	    }
  2393	   269	    starved = { pair: [a, b], bothBelowFloorEveryRun: all, status: summarizePair(blocksFor(idx, a, b, cfg.seeds, k, "d")).status, inventories };
  2394	   270	  }
  2395	   271	  const starvedOk = !plan.starved || (!!starved && starved.bothBelowFloorEveryRun && starved.status === "uninformative");
  2396	   272	  const chainT = tournament(idx, plan.chain, cfg.seeds, k);
  2397	   273	  const chainFx = chainFixture(chainT.analysis, cfg.rDirMax, plan.chain);
  2398	   274	  const fixtures = [...runFixtures({ rDirMax: cfg.rDirMax, scrambled: cfg.scrambled, panel }), chainFx];
  2399	   275	  const byId = (id: string): FixtureResult => fixtures.find((f) => f.id === id) as FixtureResult;
  2400	   276	  const assayOk = chainFx.pass && byId("strength-scrambled").pass && byId("vigour-only").pass && byId("cycle-fools-naive").pass && byId("long-cycle-fools-naive").pass && byId("strength-aligned").pass;
  2401	   277	  const sources: SourceSummary[] = plan.sources.map((ps) => {
  2402	   278	    const name = keptSources(fx).find((s) => s.short === ps.short)!.name;
  2403	   279	    const t = tournament(idx, ps.ids, cfg.seeds, k);
  2404	   280	    const L = ps.ids[ps.ids.length - 1];
  2405	   281	    const twin = twinSummary(idx, L, cfg.seeds, k, cfg.effectTarget);
  2406	   282	    const weakCopy = weakSummary(idx, L, weakId(L), cfg.seeds, k, true);
  2407	   283	    const controlInvalid = !runsValid || !twin.labelInvariance || !starvedOk;
  2408	   284	    const insensitiveOrBiased = !twin.positionOk || !gw.detected;
  2409	   285	    const { label, underlying } = temporalLabel({
  2410	   286	      controlInvalid, insensitiveOrBiased, assayFailed: !assayOk, scheduled: t.analysis.scheduled, informative: t.analysis.informative,
  2411	   287	      rDir: t.analysis.rank.rDir, rDirResid: t.analysis.residRank?.rDir ?? null, ordered: t.analysis.ordered, rDirMax: cfg.rDirMax,
  2412	   288	    });
  2413	   289	    return { short: ps.short, name, tournament: t, twin, weakCopy, label, underlying, flags: { controlInvalid, insensitiveOrBiased } };
  2414	   290	  });
  2415	   291	  const labelCounts: Dict<number> = Object.fromEntries(TEMPORAL_LABELS.map((l) => [l, 0]));
  2416	   292	  for (const s of sources) labelCounts[s.label]++;
  2417	   293	  const naiveVersusSealed = [
  2418	   294	    ...fixtures.map((f) => ({ id: f.id, naive: f.naive.fraction, label: f.label })),
  2419	   295	    ...sources.map((s) => ({ id: `source:${s.short}`, naive: s.tournament.analysis.naive.fraction, label: s.label })),
  2420	   296	  ];
  2421	   297	  return {
  2422	   298	    checkpoint: checkpoints[k], sources, gw, starved, chain: { ids: plan.chain, percents: cfg.chain.percents, tournament: chainT, fixture: chainFx }, fixtures, naiveVersusSealed,
  2423	   299	    global: { runsValid, starvedOk, gwDetected: gw.detected, assayOk }, labelCounts,
  2424	   300	  };
  2425	   301	}

  2427	   303	// ---- payload and seal ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2428	   304	
  2429	   305	export const LIMITS = [
  2430	   306	  "descriptive tournament of related surviving elites from three related bootstrap searches (all record search seed 1); no confirmatory claim, no pooled rank or p",
  2431	   307	  "archive time is the bootstrap batch index (`born`), not ecological time; the archive holds final elites, one per MAP-Elites cell, not snapshots captured during the run",
  2432	   308	  "the permutation rank r_dir is a rank against relabelled times in a genealogically related, non-exchangeable set; it is never called significant",
  2433	   309	  "the search optimised survival, recovery and light dependence, not arena competition; the arena is a 32x32 tile with gradient light, amplitude 200 and mu/sigma 154/24, unlike the search worlds (uniform light, amplitude 160, 60/20)",
  2434	   310	  "payoff is biomass share at step 300 in a small CPU arena with one chemistry; balanced arm only; rare starts are not run because they are biased against the rare side even for identical genomes",
  2435	   311	  "'nothing-beyond-null' means none detected at this resolution, never that none exists; ten seed blocks give wide intervals",
  2436	   312	  "the weakened copy of an archive genome is descriptive: the edit scales one generalist-specific weight and can leave a negative-weight genome less impaired",
  2437	   313	  "the cycle and vigour-only fixtures are synthetic; strength-not-time is a pure-matrix fixture on the first rung's committed panel matrix, with no in-world rerun",
  2438	   314	  "no fixture or control reaches directional-rank: strength-aligned and the planted chain land on directional-vigour-only, so the top label has no positive control and a real directional-rank would be unvalidated",
  2439	   315	  "no physics, schema, preset or RULE_VERSION change; no paid calls; not on the public site",
  2440	   316	];

  2442	   318	export interface Payload {
  2443	   319	  kind: "historical-opponents-archive-evidence"; version: 1;
  2444	   320	  statements: readonly string[]; note: string;
  2445	   321	  config: ArchiveConfig;
  2446	   322	  archiveSha256: string;
  2447	   323	  firstRungEvidenceSha256: string;
  2448	   324	  panel: PanelMatrix;
  2449	   325	  hex: Dict<string>;
  2450	   326	  constants: { floorDiv: number; edgeMinBlocks: number; edgeMinOffset: number; familySize: number; g2MeanMax: number; completeFraction: number; checkpoints: number[]; plant: Dict<number>; ruleVersion: number };
  2451	   327	  schedule: ScheduleCounts;
  2452	   328	  runs: RunRec[];
  2453	   329	  summary: Summary;
  2454	   330	  verdict: Dict<string>;
  2455	   331	  limits: string[];
  2456	   332	  integritySha256?: string;
  2457	   333	}
  2458	   334	export const expectedConstants = (checkpoints: number[], plant: Dict<number>, ruleVersion: number): Payload["constants"] => ({
  2459	   335	  floorDiv: FLOOR_DIV, edgeMinBlocks: EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS, edgeMinOffset: EDGE_MIN_OFFSET, familySize: FAMILY, g2MeanMax: G2_MEAN_MAX, completeFraction: COMPLETE_FRACTION.balanced, checkpoints, plant, ruleVersion,
  2460	   336	});
  2461	   337	
  2462	   338	export function buildPayload(
  2463	   339	  cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, runs: RunRec[], panel: PanelMatrix, hex: Dict<string>, archiveSha256: string, firstRungEvidenceSha256: string,
  2464	   340	  checkpoints: number[], plant: Dict<number>, ruleVersion: number,
  2465	   341	): Payload {
  2466	   342	  const sorted = [...runs].sort((x, y) => (x.key < y.key ? -1 : 1));
  2467	   343	  const bad = validateRuns({ arena: cfg.arena } as never, sorted, checkpoints);
  2468	   344	  if (bad.length) throw new Error(`invalid run records: ${bad.join("; ")}`);
  2469	   345	  const summary = summarize(cfg, fx, sorted, panel, checkpoints);
  2470	   346	  return {
  2471	   347	    kind: "historical-opponents-archive-evidence", version: 1, statements: STATEMENTS, note: SALT_NOTE, config: cfg, archiveSha256, firstRungEvidenceSha256, panel, hex,
  2472	   348	    constants: expectedConstants(checkpoints, plant, ruleVersion), schedule: scheduleCounts(planOf(cfg, fx).jobs), runs: sorted, summary, verdict: labelsOf(summary), limits: LIMITS,
  2473	   349	  };
  2474	   350	}
  2475	   351	export function sealPayload(p: Payload): Payload {
  2476	   352	  const { integritySha256: _d, ...rest } = p;
  2477	   353	  return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) };
  2478	   354	}
  2479	   355	export function verifySeal(p: Payload): boolean {
  2480	   356	  const { integritySha256, ...rest } = p;
  2481	   357	  return typeof integritySha256 === "string" && integritySha256 === sha256(canonical(rest));
  2482	   358	}
  2483	   359	
  2484	   360	/** Recompute every summary and verdict from the raw runs alone and report differences from what is stored. */
  2485	   361	export function recompute(p: Payload, fx: ArchiveFixture): string[] {
  2486	   362	  const problems: string[] = [];
  2487	   363	  try {
  2488	   364	    const bad = validateRuns({ arena: p.config.arena } as never, p.runs, p.constants.checkpoints);
  2489	   365	    if (bad.length) return bad.map((m) => `invalid run record: ${m}`);
  2490	   366	    const summary = summarize(p.config, fx, p.runs, p.panel, p.constants.checkpoints);
  2491	   367	    const norm = (x: unknown): string => canonical(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x)));
  2492	   368	    if (norm(summary) !== norm(p.summary)) problems.push("stored summary differs from the summary recomputed from the raw runs");
  2493	   369	    if (norm(labelsOf(summary)) !== norm(p.verdict)) problems.push(`stored verdict ${JSON.stringify(p.verdict)} differs from recomputed ${JSON.stringify(labelsOf(summary))}`);
  2494	   370	    if (norm(p.limits) !== norm(LIMITS)) problems.push("stored limits differ from the current limits");
  2495	   371	    if (norm(scheduleCounts(planOf(p.config, fx).jobs)) !== norm(p.schedule)) problems.push("stored schedule counts differ");
  2496	   372	  } catch (e) {
  2497	   373	    problems.push(`recompute failed: ${(e as Error).message}`);
  2498	   374	  }
  2499	   375	  return problems;
  2500	   376	}

  2553	    41	/** Frozen inventory: every scientific file. Compared byte for byte with the freeze commit. */
  2554	    42	export function frozenFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
  2555	    43	  const ls = (d: string, ext: string) => (existsSync(root + d) ? readdirSync(root + d).filter((f) => f.endsWith(ext)).sort().map((f) => d + f) : []);
  2556	    44	  return [
  2557	    45	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/archive-tournament/", ".ts"),
  2558	    46	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/opponents/", ".ts"),
  2559	    47	    ...ls("packages/schema/src/", ".ts"),
  2560	    48	    ...ls("packages/sim-ref/src/", ".ts"),
  2561	    49	    "packages/metrics/src/stats.ts",
  2562	    50	    "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/PLAN.md", "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/chain-calibration.json",
  2563	    51	    "tools/opponents/archive.ts", "tools/opponents/archive-config.json", "tools/opponents/assay.ts", "tools/opponents/boot.ts", "tools/opponents/worker.ts", "tools/opponents/config.json",
  2564	    52	    "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/archive.json", "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json",
  2565	    53	  ];
  2566	    54	}
  2567	    55	const OUTCOME_FILES = ["docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/evidence.json", "docs/historical-opponents-archive-2026-11-02/manifest.json"];
  2568	    56	const git = (root: string, args: string[]): string => execFileSync("git", args, { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8", maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }).trim();
  2569	    57	const fileAt = (root: string, commit: string, path: string): Buffer | null => { try { return execFileSync("git", ["show", `${commit}:${path}`], { cwd: root, maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }); } catch { return null; } };
  2570	    58	
  2571	    59	/** First commit that added the frozen config, unless `commit` is given. */
  2572	    60	export function freezeCommit(root = REPO, configPath = "tools/opponents/archive-config.json"): string | null {
  2573	    61	  const out = git(root, ["log", "--diff-filter=A", "--format=%H", "--", configPath]);
  2574	    62	  const lines = out.split("\n").filter(Boolean);
  2575	    63	  return lines.length ? lines[lines.length - 1] : null;
  2576	    64	}
  2577	    65	export interface FreezeResult { problems: string[]; commit: string | null; inventory: Record<string, string> }
  2578	    66	/** Verifies the freeze: ancestor of HEAD, every inventoried file byte-identical to the commit, no outcome files in it, manifest agrees. */
  2579	    67	export function freezeCheck(root = REPO, files: string[] = frozenFiles(root), commit?: string, manifestPath = MANIFEST): FreezeResult {
  2580	    68	  const problems: string[] = [];
  2581	    69	  const c = commit ?? freezeCommit(root);
  2582	    70	  if (!c) return { problems: ["no freeze commit: archive-config.json was never committed"], commit: null, inventory: {} };
  2583	    71	  try { git(root, ["merge-base", "--is-ancestor", c, "HEAD"]); } catch { problems.push(`freeze commit ${c} is not an ancestor of HEAD`); }
  2584	    72	  const tree = git(root, ["ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", c]).split("\n");
  2585	    73	  for (const o of OUTCOME_FILES) if (tree.includes(o)) problems.push(`outcome file ${o} is in the freeze commit`);
  2586	    74	  const inventory: Record<string, string> = {};
  2587	    75	  for (const f of files) {
  2588	    76	    const then = fileAt(root, c, f);
  2589	    77	    if (!then) { problems.push(`frozen file ${f} is not in the freeze commit`); continue; }
  2590	    78	    inventory[f] = sha256(then);
  2591	    79	    if (!existsSync(root + f)) problems.push(`frozen file ${f} is missing from the working tree`);
  2592	    80	    else if (sha256(readFileSync(root + f)) !== inventory[f]) problems.push(`frozen file ${f} changed after the freeze`);
  2593	    81	  }
  2594	    82	  if (existsSync(manifestPath)) {
  2595	    83	    const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
  2596	    84	    if (m.freeze?.commit !== c) problems.push("manifest freeze commit differs from the freeze commit");
  2597	    85	    else if (canonical(m.freeze.inventory) !== canonical(inventory)) problems.push("manifest freeze inventory differs from the freeze commit");
  2598	    86	  }
  2599	    87	  return { problems, commit: c, inventory };
  2600	    88	}
  2601	    89	
  2602	    90	// ---- hex map and arena params ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2603	    91	
  2604	    92	export function hexMap(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture): Record<string, string> {
  2605	    93	  const P = buildPanel(cfg.weakenPercent);
  2606	    94	  const hex: Record<string, string> = { G: P.hex.G, W: P.hex.W };
  2607	    95	  cfg.chain.percents.forEach((p, k) => { hex[`p${k}`] = genomeHex(p === 0 ? P.genomes.G : weaken(P.genomes.G, p)); });
  2608	    96	  for (const s of keptSources(fx)) {
  2609	    97	    for (const sn of s.snapshots) hex[sn.id] = sn.hex;
  2610	    98	    const L = s.snapshots[s.snapshots.length - 1];
  2611	    99	    hex[weakId(L.id)] = genomeHex(weaken(genomeFromHex(L.hex), cfg.weakenPercent));
  2612	   100	  }
  2613	   101	  return hex;
  2614	   102	}
  2615	   103	/** First rung's runJobs reads cfg.arena and cfg.depletion.override only. */
  2616	   104	const runnerCfg = (cfg: ArchiveConfig) => ({ arena: cfg.arena, depletion: { override: cfg.depletion, pair: ["", ""] } }) as never;
  2617	   105	
  2618	   106	export async function generate(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, nWorkers: number, onlyKeys?: Set<string>): Promise<{ runs: RunRec[]; hex: Record<string, string> }> {
  2619	   107	  const hex = hexMap(cfg, fx);
  2620	   108	  const jobs = planOf(cfg, fx).jobs.filter((j) => !onlyKeys || onlyKeys.has(j.key));
  2621	   109	  const runs = await runJobs(runnerCfg(cfg), jobs, hex, nWorkers);
  2622	   110	  return { runs, hex };
  2623	   111	}
  2624	   112	const arenaParams = (cfg: ArchiveConfig, depletion: boolean) => ({ ...cfg.arena, override: depletion ? cfg.depletion : undefined }) as never;
  2625	   113	
  2626	   114	export const serialize = (p: unknown): string => `${JSON.stringify(p, null, 1)}\n`;
  2627	   115	export const loadFirstRung = () => panelFromFirstRung(JSON.parse(readFileSync(FIRST_RUNG_EVIDENCE, "utf8")));
  2628	   116	
  2629	   117	export async function generatePayload(cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture, archiveText: string, nWorkers: number): Promise<Payload> {
  2630	   118	  const { runs, hex } = await generate(cfg, fx, nWorkers);
  2631	   119	  const panel = loadFirstRung();
  2632	   120	  return sealPayload(buildPayload(cfg, fx, runs, panel, hex, sha256(archiveText), sha256(readFileSync(FIRST_RUNG_EVIDENCE)), checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION));
  2633	   121	}

  2635	   123	// ---- selection (--select) ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  2637	   125	const encGenomeHex = (g: { mu: number; sigma: number; motGain: number; weights: number[]; rings?: [number, number, number] }): string =>
  2638	   126	  genomeHex({ mu: g.mu, sigma: g.sigma, motGain: g.motGain, weights: Int8Array.from(g.weights), ...(g.rings ? { rings: g.rings } : {}) });
  2639	   127	
  2640	   128	/** Decidable compatibility: the genome decodes (round trip) and the arena world built from it validates. */
  2641	   129	export function compat(cfg: ArchiveConfig, hex: string): string[] {
  2642	   130	  const problems: string[] = [];
  2643	   131	  try {
  2644	   132	    if (genomeHex(genomeFromHex(hex)) !== hex) problems.push("hex does not round-trip");
  2645	   133	    const job = { key: "compat", seed: cfg.screen.seeds[0], hexA: hex, hexB: null, slotsA: [0, 1], slotsB: [] };
  2646	   134	    const p = { ...cfg.arena };
  2647	   135	    problems.push(...validateConfig(contestConfig(job.seed, p)).map((m: string) => `config: ${m}`));
  2648	   136	    problems.push(...validateState(buildContestWorld(job, p)).map((m: string) => `state: ${m}`));
  2649	   137	  } catch (e) { problems.push((e as Error).message); }
  2650	   138	  return problems;
  2651	   139	}
  2652	   140	
  2653	   141	export const TRANSFER_CONDITIONS = [
  2654	   142	  "search worlds: 64x64 tile, uniform light (base 40, amplitude 160), mu/sigma 60/20, kernel radius 9; arena: 32x32 tile, gradient light, amplitude 200, mu/sigma 154/24 (the first rung's arena)",
  2655	   143	  "search score: survival, recovery after a 30% lesion and light dependence over 7000 steps; arena payoff: biomass share at step 300 against another label, mutation off",
  2656	   144	  "bootstrap-200 records a legacy resume with exact:false (resumed from its elites and gate passers, not byte-exact)",
  2657	   145	];
  2658	   146	
  2659	   147	export async function selectArchive(cfg: ArchiveConfig, runsDir: string, nWorkers: number): Promise<ArchiveFixture> {
  2660	   148	  const raw = cfg.sources.map((s) => {
  2661	   149	    const file = `${runsDir}/${s.name}/archive.json`;
  2662	   150	    const bytes = readFileSync(file);
  2663	   151	    const a = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));
  2664	   152	    const elites: Elite[] = a.elites.map((e: { cell: [number, number]; born: number; quality: number; genome: never }) => ({ cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: encGenomeHex(e.genome) }));
  2665	   153	    return { s, file, sha: sha256(bytes), a, elites };
  2666	   154	  });
  2667	   155	  const screens = new Map<string, { viable: boolean; b0: number[]; b300: number[] }>();
  2668	   156	  const selections = () => raw.map((r) => {
  2669	   157	    const need: string[] = [];
  2670	   158	    const sel = selectSnapshots(r.elites, cfg.bins, (e) => {
  2671	   159	      const sc = screens.get(e.hex);
  2672	   160	      if (sc) return sc.viable;
  2673	   161	      need.push(e.hex);
  2674	   162	      return true; // provisional: stops this bin until screened
  2675	   163	    });
  2676	   164	    return { sel, need };
  2677	   165	  });
  2678	   166	  for (;;) {
  2679	   167	    const sels = selections();
  2680	   168	    const need = [...new Set(sels.flatMap((x) => x.need))];
  2681	   169	    if (!need.length) break;
  2682	   170	    const hex: Record<string, string> = {};
  2683	   171	    const jobs: JobSpec[] = [];
  2684	   172	    need.forEach((h, i) => {
  2685	   173	      hex[`g${i}`] = h;
  2686	   174	      for (const seed of cfg.screen.seeds) jobs.push({ key: `s|g${i}|m01|${seed}`, seed, a: `g${i}`, b: null, slotsA: [0, 1], slotsB: [], depletion: false });
  2687	   175	    });
  2688	   176	    const runs = await runJobs(runnerCfg(cfg), jobs, hex, nWorkers);
  2689	   177	    const byKey = new Map(runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
  2690	   178	    need.forEach((h, i) => {
  2691	   179	      const rs = cfg.screen.seeds.map((seed) => byKey.get(`s|g${i}|m01|${seed}`) as RunRec);
  2692	   180	      const b0 = rs.map((r) => r.b0[0]), b300 = rs.map((r) => r.cp[r.cp.length - 1][0]);
  2693	   181	      screens.set(h, { viable: rs.every((r) => !atFloor(r.cp[r.cp.length - 1][0], r.b0[0])), b0, b300 });
  2694	   182	    });
  2695	   183	    console.error(`screened ${need.length} genomes (${screens.size} total)`);
  2696	   184	  }
  2697	   185	  const final = selections();
  2698	   186	  const sources: SourceFixture[] = [];
  2699	   187	  const seenHex = new Map<string, string>();
  2700	   188	  raw.forEach((r, si) => {
  2701	   189	    const { sel } = final[si];
  2702	   190	    const snaps: SnapshotRec[] = sel.bins.filter((b) => b.pick).map((b) => {
  2703	   191	      const e = b.pick as Elite;
  2704	   192	      return { id: `${r.s.short}:${b.bin}`, bin: b.bin, cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: e.hex, sharedWith: [] };
  2705	   193	    });
  2706	   194	    for (const sn of snaps) {
  2707	   195	      const prior = seenHex.get(sn.hex);
  2708	   196	      if (prior) sn.sharedWith.push(prior);
  2709	   197	    }
  2710	   198	    snaps.forEach((sn) => { if (!seenHex.has(sn.hex)) seenHex.set(sn.hex, sn.id); });
  2711	   199	    const problems = snaps.flatMap((sn) => compat(cfg, sn.hex).map((m) => `${sn.id}: ${m}`));
  2712	   200	    let status: SourceFixture["status"] = "kept", dropReason: string | null = null;
  2713	   201	    if (viableBins(sel.bins) < cfg.minViableBins) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = `fewer than ${cfg.minViableBins} viable bins (${viableBins(sel.bins)})`; }
  2714	   202	    else if (si > 0 && snaps.every((sn) => sn.sharedWith.length > 0)) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = "every snapshot is byte-identical to a snapshot of an earlier source"; }
  2715	   203	    else if (problems.length) { status = "dropped"; dropReason = `incompatible with the arena: ${problems[0]}`; }
  2716	   204	    const resumes = r.a.resumes as { from: number; exact: boolean }[] | undefined;
  2717	   205	    sources.push({
  2718	   206	      name: r.s.name, short: r.s.short, file: `runs/${r.s.name}/archive.json`, fileSha256: r.sha, evaluated: r.a.evaluated, elites: r.elites.length, horizon: sel.horizon,
  2719	   207	      resumeExact: resumes ? resumes.every((x) => x.exact) : null,
  2720	   208	      bins: sel.bins.map((b) => ({ bin: b.bin, lo: b.lo, hi: b.hi, status: b.status, candidates: b.candidates, screened: b.screened, snapshot: b.pick ? `${r.s.short}:${b.bin}` : null })),
  2721	   209	      snapshots: snaps, status, dropReason, compat: { ok: problems.length === 0, problems },
  2722	   210	      screens: [...new Set(sel.bins.flatMap((b) => (b.pick ? [b.pick.hex] : [])))].map((h) => ({ hex: h, ...(screens.get(h) as { viable: boolean; b0: number[]; b300: number[] }) })),
  2723	   211	    });
  2724	   212	  });
  2725	   213	  return { kind: "historical-opponents-archive-fixture", version: 1, transferConditions: TRANSFER_CONDITIONS, screenSeeds: cfg.screen.seeds, sources };
  2726	   214	}

  2730	   218	export function sourceFiles(root = REPO): string[] {
  2731	   219	  const ls = (d: string) => (existsSync(root + d) ? readdirSync(root + d).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".ts")).sort().map((f) => d + f) : []);
  2732	   220	  return [
  2733	   221	    ...ls("packages/metrics/src/archive-tournament/"), ...ls("packages/metrics/src/opponents/"), ...ls("packages/schema/src/"), ...ls("packages/sim-ref/src/"),
  2734	   222	    "packages/metrics/src/stats.ts", "tools/opponents/archive.ts", "tools/opponents/archive-config.json", "tools/opponents/assay.ts", "tools/opponents/worker.ts", "tools/opponents/boot.ts", "tools/opponents/config.json",
  2735	   223	  ];
  2736	   224	}
  2737	   225	const sourceHashes = (root = REPO): Record<string, string> => Object.fromEntries(sourceFiles(root).map((f) => [f, sha256(readFileSync(root + f))]));
  2738	   226	
  2739	   227	export function makeManifest(payload: Payload, evidenceText: string, freeze: FreezeResult, cpuSeconds: number | null): Record<string, unknown> {
  2740	   228	  return {
  2741	   229	    kind: "historical-opponents-archive-manifest",
  2742	   230	    payloadFileSha256: sha256(evidenceText),
  2743	   231	    integritySha256: payload.integritySha256,
  2744	   232	    archiveSha256: payload.archiveSha256,
  2745	   233	    config: payload.config,
  2746	   234	    constants: payload.constants,
  2747	   235	    schedule: payload.schedule,
  2748	   236	    verdict: payload.verdict,
  2749	   237	    freeze: { commit: freeze.commit, inventory: freeze.inventory },
  2750	   238	    sources: sourceHashes(),
  2751	   239	    provenance: { note: "not replayed; excluded from the deterministic payload", node: process.version, platform: `${platform()}-${arch()}`, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(), cpuSeconds },
  2752	   240	  };
  2753	   241	}
  2754	   242	
  2755	   243	export const expectedHex = (cfg: ArchiveConfig, fx: ArchiveFixture) => hexMap(cfg, fx);
  2756	   244	
  2757	   245	/** Compare the archive fixture with the source files when `runsDir` is given; otherwise only internal consistency. Returns problems and a skip note. */
  2758	   246	export function checkFixture(fx: ArchiveFixture, cfg: ArchiveConfig, runsDir: string | null): { problems: string[]; note: string | null } {
  2759	   247	  const problems: string[] = [];
  2760	   248	  for (const s of fx.sources) {
  2761	   249	    for (const sc of s.screens) {
  2762	   250	      const ints = (v: unknown): v is number[] => Array.isArray(v) && v.length === cfg.screen.seeds.length && v.every((x) => Number.isInteger(x) && x >= 0);
  2763	   251	      if (!ints(sc.b0) || !ints(sc.b300) || sc.b0.some((x) => x <= 0)) problems.push(`${s.short}: screen record for ${sc.hex.slice(0, 12)} has malformed counts`);
  2764	   252	      else if (sc.viable !== sc.b300.every((b, i) => !atFloor(b, sc.b0[i]))) problems.push(`${s.short}: screen record for ${sc.hex.slice(0, 12)} has a viable flag that contradicts its counts`);
  2765	   253	    }
  2766	   254	    const viableHex = new Set(s.screens.filter((x) => x.viable).map((x) => x.hex));
  2767	   255	    for (const sn of s.snapshots) {
  2768	   256	      if (!viableHex.has(sn.hex)) problems.push(`${sn.id}: snapshot has no passing viability screen`);
  2769	   257	      if (compat(cfg, sn.hex).length) problems.push(`${sn.id}: snapshot is not arena-compatible`);
  2770	   258	    }
  2771	   259	  }
  2772	   260	  if (canonical(fx.screenSeeds) !== canonical(cfg.screen.seeds)) problems.push("fixture screen seeds differ from the config");
  2773	   261	  if (!runsDir) return { problems, note: "source-hash check skipped: RUNS is not set (runs/ exists only in the main checkout)" };
  2774	   262	  for (const s of fx.sources) {
  2775	   263	    const file = `${runsDir}/${s.name}/archive.json`;
  2776	   264	    if (!existsSync(file)) { problems.push(`${s.name}: source file missing under RUNS`); continue; }
  2777	   265	    const bytes = readFileSync(file);
  2778	   266	    if (sha256(bytes) !== s.fileSha256) { problems.push(`${s.name}: source file SHA-256 differs from the fixture`); continue; }
  2779	   267	    const a = JSON.parse(bytes.toString("utf8"));
  2780	   268	    const elites: Elite[] = a.elites.map((e: { cell: [number, number]; born: number; quality: number; genome: never }) => ({ cell: e.cell, born: e.born, quality: e.quality, hex: encGenomeHex(e.genome) }));
  2781	   269	    const screened = new Map(s.screens.map((x) => [x.hex, x.viable]));
  2782	   270	    const sel = selectSnapshots(elites, cfg.bins, (e) => screened.get(e.hex) ?? false);
  2783	   271	    if (sel.horizon !== s.horizon) problems.push(`${s.name}: horizon differs`);
  2784	   272	    for (const b of sel.bins) {
  2785	   273	      const rec = s.bins[b.bin];
  2786	   274	      const want = b.pick ? `${s.short}:${b.bin}` : null;
  2787	   275	      if (rec.snapshot !== want) problems.push(`${s.name}: bin ${b.bin} selection differs from the recomputed one`);
  2788	   276	      if (b.pick && s.snapshots.find((x) => x.id === want)?.hex !== b.pick.hex) problems.push(`${s.name}: bin ${b.bin} genome differs`);
  2789	   277	    }
  2790	   278	  }
  2791	   279	  return { problems, note: null };
  2792	   280	}
  2793	   281	
  2794	   282	/** Validate saved artifacts without simulating. */
  2795	   283	export function checkSaved(o: { evidencePath?: string; manifestPath?: string; archivePath?: string; configPath?: string; root?: string; runsDir?: string | null } = {}): string[] {
  2796	   284	  const evidencePath = o.evidencePath ?? EVIDENCE, manifestPath = o.manifestPath ?? MANIFEST, archivePath = o.archivePath ?? ARCHIVE, root = o.root ?? REPO;
  2797	   285	  const problems: string[] = [];
  2798	   286	  if (!existsSync(evidencePath) || !existsSync(manifestPath)) return ["evidence.json or manifest.json is missing"];
  2799	   287	  const text = readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8");
  2800	   288	  const payload = JSON.parse(text) as Payload;
  2801	   289	  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(manifestPath, "utf8"));
  2802	   290	  const archiveText = readFileSync(archivePath, "utf8");
  2803	   291	  const fx = JSON.parse(archiveText) as ArchiveFixture;
  2804	   292	  const cfg: ArchiveConfig = JSON.parse(readFileSync(o.configPath ?? CONFIG_PATH, "utf8"));
  2805	   293	  if (!verifySeal(payload)) problems.push("integritySha256 does not match the payload");
  2806	   294	  if (manifest.payloadFileSha256 !== sha256(text)) problems.push("manifest payloadFileSha256 does not match evidence.json");
  2807	   295	  if (manifest.integritySha256 !== payload.integritySha256) problems.push("manifest seal differs from the payload seal");
  2808	   296	  if (manifest.archiveSha256 !== sha256(archiveText) || payload.archiveSha256 !== sha256(archiveText)) problems.push("archive.json changed since the evidence was written");
  2809	   297	  if (canonical(manifest.config) !== canonical(payload.config)) problems.push("manifest config differs from the payload");
  2810	   298	  if (canonical(manifest.verdict) !== canonical(payload.verdict)) problems.push("manifest verdict differs from the payload");
  2811	   299	  if (canonical(payload.config) !== canonical(cfg)) problems.push("payload config differs from the frozen archive-config.json");
  2812	   300	  if (JSON.stringify(payload.statements) !== JSON.stringify(STATEMENTS)) problems.push("statements differ");
  2813	   301	  if (payload.note !== SALT_NOTE) problems.push("payload note differs");
  2814	   302	  if (payload.kind !== "historical-opponents-archive-evidence" || payload.version !== 1) problems.push("payload kind or version differs");
  2815	   303	  if (canonical(payload.limits) !== canonical(LIMITS)) problems.push("payload limits differ");
  2816	   304	  if (payload.firstRungEvidenceSha256 !== sha256(readFileSync(root + "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json"))) problems.push("first rung evidence.json changed");
  2817	   305	  const listed = Object.keys(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>);
  2818	   306	  for (const f of sourceFiles(root)) if (!listed.includes(f)) problems.push(`source ${f} is not in the manifest (added since the evidence was written)`);
  2819	   307	  for (const [file, hash] of Object.entries(manifest.sources as Record<string, string>)) {
  2820	   308	    if (!existsSync(root + file)) problems.push(`source ${file} is missing`);
  2821	   309	    else if (sha256(readFileSync(root + file)) !== hash) problems.push(`source ${file} changed since the evidence was written`);
  2822	   310	  }
  2823	   311	  const cmp = (name: string, want: unknown, got: unknown) => { if (canonical(want) !== canonical(got ?? null)) problems.push(`${name} differs from the value reconstructed from the frozen inputs`); };
  2824	   312	  cmp("payload hex map", hexMap(cfg, fx), payload.hex);
  2825	   313	  cmp("payload panel", loadFirstRungAt(root), payload.panel);
  2826	   314	  cmp("payload constants", expectedConstants(checkpointSteps(cfg.arena.steps), { ...PLANT }, RULE_VERSION), payload.constants);
  2827	   315	  cmp("payload schedule counts", scheduleCounts(planOf(cfg, fx).jobs), payload.schedule);
  2828	   316	  cmp("manifest constants", payload.constants, manifest.constants);
  2829	   317	  cmp("manifest schedule", payload.schedule, manifest.schedule);
  2830	   318	  if (root === REPO) problems.push(...freezeCheck(REPO, undefined, undefined, manifestPath).problems);
  2831	   319	  else if (!manifest.freeze?.commit || !/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(manifest.freeze.commit)) problems.push("manifest has no valid freeze commit");
  2832	   320	  const fixture = checkFixture(fx, cfg, o.runsDir === undefined ? (process.env.RUNS ?? null) : o.runsDir);
  2833	   321	  problems.push(...fixture.problems);
  2834	   322	  problems.push(...recompute(payload, fx));
  2835	   323	  problems.push(...checkStepZero(payload, fx));
  2836	   324	  return problems;
  2837	   325	}
  2838	   326	const loadFirstRungAt = (root: string) => panelFromFirstRung(JSON.parse(readFileSync(root + "docs/historical-opponents-2026-11-01/evidence.json", "utf8")));

  2840	   328	/** Rebuild every run's step-zero world (no simulation step) and compare the stored inventories with it. */
  2841	   329	export function checkStepZero(payload: Payload, fx: ArchiveFixture): string[] {
  2842	   330	  const out: string[] = [];
  2843	   331	  const stored = new Map(payload.runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
  2844	   332	  for (const j of planOf(payload.config, fx).jobs) {
  2845	   333	    const r = stored.get(j.key);
  2846	   334	    if (!r) continue;
  2847	   335	    const job = { key: j.key, seed: j.seed, hexA: payload.hex[j.a.replace(/'$/, "")], hexB: j.b === null ? null : payload.hex[j.b.replace(/'$/, "")], slotsA: j.slotsA, slotsB: j.slotsB };
  2848	   336	    const p = arenaParams(payload.config, j.depletion);
  2849	   337	    const z = stepZero(buildContestWorld(job, p), job, p);
  2850	   338	    if (r.b0[0] !== z.b0A || r.b0[1] !== z.b0B || r.e0[0] !== z.e0A || r.e0[1] !== z.e0B) { out.push(`${j.key}: stored step-zero inventories differ from the rebuilt world`); if (out.length >= 10) break; }
  2851	   339	  }
  2852	   340	  return out;
  2853	   341	}
  2854	   342	
  2855	   343	/** Deterministic sample of jobs: FNV-1a of the key modulo 100 below `pct`. */
  2856	   344	export const sampled = (key: string, pct: number): boolean => { let h = 2166136261; for (const c of key) { h ^= c.charCodeAt(0); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619) >>> 0; } return h % 100 < pct; };
  2857	   345	
  2858	   346	export async function replayCheck(nWorkers: number, pct = 100): Promise<string[]> {
  2859	   347	  // saved evidence, seal, manifest and freeze provenance are verified first, in full, before any simulation is spent
  2860	   348	  if (!existsSync(EVIDENCE) || !existsSync(MANIFEST)) return ["evidence.json or manifest.json is missing: nothing to replay (and no scoring seed is spent)"];
  2861	   349	  const pre = checkSaved();
  2862	   350	  if (pre.length) return pre;
  2863	   351	  const archiveText = readFileSync(ARCHIVE, "utf8");
  2864	   352	  const fx = JSON.parse(archiveText) as ArchiveFixture;
  2865	   353	  if (pct >= 100) {
  2866	   354	    const fresh = serialize(await generatePayload(CONFIG, fx, archiveText, nWorkers));
  2867	   355	    return fresh === readFileSync(EVIDENCE, "utf8") ? [] : ["regenerated payload differs from evidence.json"];
  2868	   356	  }
  2869	   357	  const saved = JSON.parse(readFileSync(EVIDENCE, "utf8")) as Payload;
  2870	   358	  const keys = new Set(planOf(CONFIG, fx).jobs.map((j) => j.key).filter((k) => sampled(k, pct)));
  2871	   359	  const { runs } = await generate(CONFIG, fx, nWorkers, keys);
  2872	   360	  const byKey = new Map(saved.runs.map((r) => [r.key, r]));
  2873	   361	  const bad = runs.filter((r) => canonical(r) !== canonical(byKey.get(r.key)));
  2874	   362	  console.log(`replayed ${runs.length} of ${saved.runs.length} jobs (${pct}% deterministic sample)`);
  2875	   363	  return bad.length ? [`${bad.length} replayed run records differ from evidence.json (first ${bad[0].key})`] : [];
  2876	   364	}

  2897	   385	function startCpuGuard(): () => number {
  2898	   386	  const t = setInterval(() => { const u = process.cpuUsage(); if ((u.user + u.system) / 1e6 > CPU_CAP_SECONDS) { console.error(`aborting: cumulative CPU exceeded ${CPU_CAP_SECONDS} s`); process.exit(3); } }, 5000);
  2899	   387	  t.unref();

/**
 * The planted chain is in-world: classify it from its measured matrix. Must be directional (rank or vigour-only): the planted
 * manipulation is a weakened photosynthesis weight, a vigour manipulation, so its residual need not align (calibration on the
 * screen seeds showed r_dir = 1/24 with residual ranks 0.29 to 0.67). Every scheduled chain pair must be informative. The vigour split is gated by the vigour-only fixture.
 */
export function chainFixture(a: Analysis, rDirMax: number, times: string[]): FixtureResult {
  return result("planted-chain", false, times, a, rDirMax, "directional-rank or directional-vigour-only", a.informative === a.scheduled && ["directional-rank", "directional-vigour-only"].includes(bodyOf(a, rDirMax)), "calibrated instrument genomes with graded weakening, times in order of strength (in-world positive control)");
}

/** Search used at freeze time: the first order (lexicographic over indices into the strength order) with rank above 0.5 that has the highest naive fraction. */
export function chooseScrambled(panel: PanelMatrix, rDirMax: number): { order: string[]; rDir: number; naive: number } | null {
  const base = strengthOrder(panel);
  let best: { order: string[]; rDir: number; naive: number } | null = null;
  for (const p of permutations(base.length)) {
    const order = p.map((k) => base[k]);
    const a = analyze(panelUnderOrder(panel, order));
    const rd = a.rank.rDir ?? 0;
    if (rd <= 0.2 || a.cycle || !a.ordered || rd <= rDirMax) continue;
    if (!best || a.naive.fraction > best.naive) best = { order, rDir: rd, naive: a.naive.fraction };
  }
  return best;
}

---
name: code-review
description: Review the changes since a fixed point (commit, branch, tag, or merge-base) along two axes — Standards (does the code follow this repo's documented coding standards?) and Spec (does the code match what the originating issue/PRD asked for?). Runs both reviews in parallel sub-agents and reports them side by side. Use when the user wants to review a branch, a PR, work-in-progress changes, or asks to "review since X".
---

Two-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:

- **Standards** — does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?
- **Spec** — does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / PRD / spec?

Both axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.

The issue tracker should have been provided to you — run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills` if `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing.

## Process

### 1. Pin the fixed point

Whatever the user said is the fixed point — a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc. If they didn't specify one, ask for it.

Capture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.

Before going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here — not inside two parallel sub-agents.

### 2. Identify the spec source

Look for the originating spec, in this order:

     1	// Pure aggregation for the opponent assay: run classes, shares from integer counts, exact sign-flip tests, intervals,
     2	// invasion, vigour residual, cycles, verdict. Integers in, floats only in the final statistics. No simulator imports.
     3	import { mean } from "../stats.ts";
     4	
     5	export const FLOOR_DIV = 16;
     6	/** A label is at the floor when its biomass is below 1/16 of its own step-zero biomass (integer test). */
     7	export const atFloor = (b: number, b0: number): boolean => b * FLOOR_DIV < b0;
     8	
     9	export type RunClass = "contested" | "decisive" | "both-floor";
    10	export function classify(b0A: number, b0B: number, bA: number, bB: number): RunClass {
    11	  const fa = atFloor(bA, b0A), fb = atFloor(bB, b0B);
    12	  return fa && fb ? "both-floor" : fa || fb ? "decisive" : "contested";
    13	}
    14	/** Share of A from integer counts. Null when the run is `both-floor` (undefined, never 0.5) or there is no biomass. */
    15	export function shareA(b0A: number, b0B: number, bA: number, bB: number): number | null {
    16	  if (classify(b0A, b0B, bA, bB) === "both-floor" || bA + bB === 0) return null;
    17	  return bA / (bA + bB);
    18	}
    19	/** Display value only; never feeds a gate. */
    20	export const sharePpm = (bA: number, bB: number): number | null => (bA + bB === 0 ? null : Math.floor((1e6 * bA) / (bA + bB)));
    21	
    22	/** Two-sided 95% t quantiles, df 1..9 (frozen table; S <= 10 blocks). */
    23	export const T95 = [12.706, 4.303, 3.182, 2.776, 2.571, 2.447, 2.365, 2.306, 2.262] as const;
    24	
    25	/** Exact two-sided sign-flip p of the sum of `d` (null for an empty input): fraction of the 2^n sign assignments whose |sum| >= observed. */
    26	export function signFlipP(d: readonly number[]): number | null {
    27	  const n = d.length;
    28	  if (n === 0) return null;
    29	  if (n > 20) throw new Error("signFlipP: n > 20");
    30	  const obs = Math.abs(d.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0));
    31	  const eps = 1e-12;
    32	  let ge = 0;
    33	  for (let m = 0; m < 1 << n; m++) {
    34	    let s = 0;
    35	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) s += m & (1 << i) ? d[i] : -d[i];
    36	    if (Math.abs(s) >= obs - eps) ge++;
    37	  }
    38	  return ge / 2 ** n;
    39	}
    40	export const minP = (n: number): number => 2 / 2 ** n;
    41	
    42	export interface Interval { mean: number; lo: number; hi: number; df: number }
    43	/** Descriptive t interval (df = n - 1). Never decides anything. Null for n < 2. */
    44	export function tInterval(x: readonly number[]): Interval | null {
    45	  const n = x.length;
    46	  if (n < 2) return null;
    47	  if (n - 1 > T95.length) throw new Error("tInterval: more blocks than the frozen table covers");
    48	  const m = mean([...x]);
    49	  const sd = Math.sqrt(x.reduce((a, v) => a + (v - m) ** 2, 0) / (n - 1));
    50	  const h = (T95[n - 2] * sd) / Math.sqrt(n);
    51	  return { mean: m, lo: m - h, hi: m + h, df: n - 1 };
    52	}
    53	
    54	/** Per-seed record of one contest world at one checkpoint. */
    55	export interface Obs { b0A: number; b0B: number; bA: number; bB: number }
    56	
    57	export interface BlockPayoff {
    58	  seed: number;
    59	  /** Mean of A's share over both positions; null when either position is both-floor (the whole block is dropped). */
    60	  payoff: number | null;
    61	  /** Both-floor counted as 0.5 and nothing dropped: the naive reading. */
    62	  naive: number;
    63	  shares: (number | null)[];
    64	}
    65	export function blockPayoff(seed: number, positions: readonly Obs[]): BlockPayoff {
    66	  const shares = positions.map((o) => shareA(o.b0A, o.b0B, o.bA, o.bB));
    67	  const payoff = shares.every((s) => s !== null) ? mean(shares as number[]) : null;
    68	  const naive = mean(positions.map((o, i) => shares[i] ?? 0.5));
    69	  return { seed, payoff, naive, shares };
    70	}
    71	
    72	export type PairStatus = "informative" | "uninformative";
    73	export interface PairSummary {
    74	  blocks: number;
    75	  complete: number;
    76	  excluded: number;
    77	  payoffs: number[];
    78	  seeds: number[];
    79	  mean: number | null;
    80	  interval: Interval | null;
    81	  signFlipP: number | null;
    82	  status: PairStatus;
    83	  naiveMean: number;
    84	  /** "A>B", "B>A" or null. Only the confirmatory family may use it. */
    85	  edge: "A>B" | "B>A" | null;
    86	  allSameSide: boolean;
    87	}
    88	export const EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS = 9;
    89	export const EDGE_MIN_OFFSET = 0.05;
    90	export const FAMILY = 10;
    91	export const COMPLETE_FRACTION = { balanced: 0.8, rare: 5 / 6 };
    92	
    93	export function summarizePair(blocks: readonly BlockPayoff[], minFraction = COMPLETE_FRACTION.balanced): PairSummary {
    94	  const done = blocks.filter((b) => b.payoff !== null);
    95	  const payoffs = done.map((b) => b.payoff as number);
    96	  const d = payoffs.map((p) => p - 0.5);
    97	  const n = payoffs.length;
    98	  const m = n ? mean(payoffs) : null;
    99	  const status: PairStatus = n >= minFraction * blocks.length - 1e-9 && n > 0 ? "informative" : "uninformative";
   100	  const pv = signFlipP(d);
   101	  const allSameSide = n > 0 && (d.every((v) => v > 0) || d.every((v) => v < 0));
   102	  let edge: PairSummary["edge"] = null;
   103	  if (status === "informative" && allSameSide && n >= EDGE_MIN_BLOCKS && pv !== null && pv <= 0.05 / FAMILY && m !== null && Math.abs(m - 0.5) >= EDGE_MIN_OFFSET) edge = m > 0.5 ? "A>B" : "B>A";
   104	  return {
   105	    blocks: blocks.length, complete: n, excluded: blocks.length - n, payoffs, seeds: done.map((b) => b.seed), mean: m, interval: tInterval(payoffs), signFlipP: pv, status,
   106	    naiveMean: mean(blocks.map((b) => b.naive)), edge, allSameSide,
   107	  };
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** Invasion from rare: change of the rare label's share from its measured initial share, per block (mean over the two positions). */
   111	export type InvasionClass = "invades" | "fails" | "unclear" | "uninformative";
   112	export interface InvasionSummary { excess: number[]; complete: number; blocks: number; signFlipP: number | null; class: InvasionClass }
   113	export function summarizeInvasion(perBlock: readonly (number | null)[], minComplete = 5): InvasionSummary {
   114	  const ex = perBlock.filter((v): v is number => v !== null);
   115	  const pv = signFlipP(ex);
   116	  let cls: InvasionClass = "unclear";
   117	  if (ex.length < minComplete) cls = "uninformative";
   118	  else if (ex.every((v) => v > 0) && ex.length === perBlock.length) cls = "invades";
   119	  else if (ex.every((v) => v < 0) && ex.length === perBlock.length) cls = "fails";
   120	  return { excess: ex, complete: ex.length, blocks: perBlock.length, signFlipP: pv, class: cls };
   121	}
   122	
   123	/** Descriptive monoculture residual: observed share minus the share solo growth at the same positions predicts. */
   124	export function vigourExpected(vA: number, vB: number): number | null { return vA + vB === 0 ? null : vA / (vA + vB); }
   125	export const vigourResidual = (observed: number, expected: number | null): number | null => (expected === null ? null : observed - expected);
   126	
   127	/** Directed 3-cycles among confirmatory edges. edges[i][j] true means i beats j. Each cycle once, rotated to start at its smallest index. */
   128	export function findCycles(edges: readonly (readonly boolean[])[]): number[][] {
   129	  const n = edges.length, out: number[][] = [];
   130	  for (let a = 0; a < n; a++) for (let b = 0; b < n; b++) for (let c = 0; c < n; c++) {
   131	    if (a === b || b === c || a === c) continue;
   132	    if (a < b && a < c && edges[a][b] && edges[b][c] && edges[c][a]) out.push([a, b, c]);
   133	  }
   134	  return out;
   135	}
   136	/** True when there is no directed 3-cycle among the edges. This is NOT a transitivity test: a longer cycle (A>B>C>D>A) passes. */
   137	export function hasNoThreeCycle(edges: readonly (readonly boolean[])[]): boolean {
   138	  return findCycles(edges).length === 0;
   139	}
   140	/** Edge matrix from a payoff matrix: A beats B when the payoff is above 0.5 by at least `offset` and the cell is marked decisive. */
   141	export function edgesFromPayoffs(p: readonly (readonly (number | null)[])[], offset = EDGE_MIN_OFFSET): boolean[][] {
   142	  return p.map((row, i) => row.map((v, j) => i !== j && v !== null && v - 0.5 >= offset));
   143	}
   144	
   145	/** Breadth: opponents beaten by a confirmatory edge. Reported beside mean payoff, never combined. */
   146	export function breadth(edges: readonly (readonly boolean[])[]): number[] { return edges.map((row) => row.filter(Boolean).length); }
   147	
   148	export interface Gates { G1a: boolean; G1b: boolean; G2: boolean; G3: boolean; G4: boolean; G5: boolean; invalid: boolean }
   149	export type Verdict = "assay-sensitive" | "label-or-position-biased" | "insensitive" | "extinction-limited" | "control-invalid";
   150	/** Precedence is fixed: control-invalid, then label-or-position-biased, insensitive, extinction-limited, assay-sensitive. */
   151	export function decideVerdict(g: Gates): Verdict {
   152	  if (g.invalid || !g.G4 || !g.G5) return "control-invalid";
   153	  if (!g.G1a || !g.G1b) return "label-or-position-biased";
   154	  if (!g.G2) return "insensitive";
   155	  if (!g.G3) return "extinction-limited";
   156	  return "assay-sensitive";
   157	}
